import os
import py_compile

PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def write_and_verify(rel_path: str, content: str):
    full_path = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, rel_path)
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(full_path), exist_ok=True)
    with open(full_path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
        f.write(content.lstrip())
    if full_path.endswith(".py"):
        py_compile.compile(full_path, doraise=True)
    print(f"[RESTORED & VERIFIED] {rel_path}")


# ==============================================================================
# 1. RESTORE EXACT LIVE cvd.py (window=500, strict mid-price aggression check)
# ==============================================================================
cvd_code = r'''
from collections import deque
from .base import BaseIndicator

class CumulativeVolumeDelta(BaseIndicator):
    def __init__(self, window: int = 500):
        super().__init__("CVD")
        self.window = window
        self._deltas = {}

    def update(self, tick: dict):
        sec_id = str(tick.get('security_id'))
        self._initialize_state(sec_id)

        if sec_id not in self._deltas:
            self._deltas[sec_id] = deque(maxlen=self.window)

        ltp = tick.get('ltp', 0.0)
        ltq = tick.get('ltq', 0)
        bid = tick.get('bid', 0.0)
        ask = tick.get('ask', 0.0)

        if not sec_id or bid == 0.0 or ask == 0.0 or ltp == 0.0 or ltq == 0:
            return

        mid = (bid + ask) / 2.0

        if ltp > mid:
            delta = ltq
        elif ltp < mid:
            delta = -ltq
        else:
            delta = 0

        self._deltas[sec_id].append(delta)
        self.values[sec_id] = sum(self._deltas[sec_id])
'''
write_and_verify("src/trading/indicators/cvd.py", cvd_code)


# ==============================================================================
# 2. RESTORE EXACT LIVE obi.py (alpha=0.10 EMA smoothing, pure book volume ratio)
# ==============================================================================
obi_code = r'''
from .base import BaseIndicator

class OrderBookImbalance(BaseIndicator):
    def __init__(self, alpha: float = 0.1):
        super().__init__("OBI")
        self.alpha = alpha
        self._raw_obi = {}

    def update(self, tick: dict):
        sec_id = str(tick.get('security_id'))
        self._initialize_state(sec_id)

        bid_vol = tick.get('best_bid_vol', 0)
        ask_vol = tick.get('best_ask_vol', 0)

        total_vol = bid_vol + ask_vol

        if total_vol == 0:
            raw_obi = 0.0
        else:
            raw_obi = (bid_vol - ask_vol) / total_vol

        if sec_id not in self._raw_obi:
            self._raw_obi[sec_id] = raw_obi
        else:
            self._raw_obi[sec_id] = (self.alpha * raw_obi) + ((1.0 - self.alpha) * self._raw_obi[sec_id])

        self.values[sec_id] = self._raw_obi[sec_id]
'''
write_and_verify("src/trading/indicators/obi.py", obi_code)

# ==============================================================================
# 4. RESTORE LIVE WINNING MATH IN src/trading/portfolio.py
#    - Exact 20% max notional allocation cap (Rs.20,000 max per position)
#    - Keeps Max Concurrent Positions (default 4) & Active/Idle Capital telemetry
# ==============================================================================
portfolio_code = r'''
import json
import os

class PortfolioManager:
    def __init__(
        self,
        starting_balance: float = 100000.0,
        max_allocation_pct: float = 0.20,
        max_loss_pct: float = 0.02,
        max_concurrent_positions: int = None,
        state_file: str = "portfolio_state.json"
    ):
        self.state_file = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), state_file)
        loaded_balance = self._load_state(starting_balance)

        self.initial_balance = loaded_balance
        self.current_balance = loaded_balance
        self.max_allocation_pct = max_allocation_pct
        self.max_loss_pct = max_loss_pct
        self.max_concurrent_positions = int(
            max_concurrent_positions or os.getenv("MAX_CONCURRENT_POSITIONS", 4)
        )

        self.positions = {}
        self.daily_pnl = 0.0
        self.peak_capital_deployed = 0.0
        self.total_turnover_deployed = 0.0

    def _load_state(self, default_balance: float) -> float:
        if os.path.exists(self.state_file):
            try:
                with open(self.state_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    return float(data.get("current_balance", default_balance))
            except Exception:
                pass
        return default_balance

    def save_state(self):
        with open(self.state_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump({
                "current_balance": round(self.current_balance, 2),
                "initial_balance": round(self.initial_balance, 2),
                "daily_pnl": round(self.daily_pnl, 2),
                "peak_capital_deployed": round(self.peak_capital_deployed, 2),
                "total_turnover_deployed": round(self.total_turnover_deployed, 2),
                "max_concurrent_positions": self.max_concurrent_positions
            }, f, indent=4)

    def get_active_notional(self) -> float:
        return sum(pos['qty'] * pos['avg_price'] for pos in self.positions.values())

    def get_margin_used(self) -> float:
        return self.get_active_notional() / 5.0

    def get_idle_capital(self) -> float:
        return max(0.0, self.current_balance - self.get_margin_used())

    def can_take_trade(self, sec_id, action: str, requested_notional: float) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        sec_id = str(sec_id)
        if self.daily_pnl <= -(self.initial_balance * self.max_loss_pct):
            return False, "REJECTED_MAX_DAILY_DRAWDOWN"

        if sec_id not in self.positions and len(self.positions) >= self.max_concurrent_positions:
            return False, f"REJECTED_MAX_SLOTS_{self.max_concurrent_positions}"

        # Exact live portfolio limit: single position notional <= current_balance * max_allocation_pct (20%)
        if requested_notional > (self.current_balance * self.max_allocation_pct):
            return False, "REJECTED_PORTFOLIO_LIMITS"

        if (requested_notional / 5.0) > self.get_idle_capital():
            return False, "REJECTED_INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS"

        return True, "OK"

    def update_position(self, sec_id, action: str, qty: int, fill_price: float, symbol: str = "", trigger_source: str = "") -> float:
        sec_id = str(sec_id)
        pos = self.positions.get(sec_id)
        realized_pnl = 0.0
        trade_notional = float(qty * fill_price)

        if not pos:
            self.positions[sec_id] = {
                'side': action,
                'qty': qty,
                'avg_price': fill_price,
                'symbol': symbol or f"TOK_{sec_id}",
                'trigger_source': trigger_source or "BOT_MATH"
            }
            self.total_turnover_deployed += trade_notional
        elif pos['side'] == action:
            total_qty = pos['qty'] + qty
            pos['avg_price'] = ((pos['avg_price'] * pos['qty']) + (fill_price * qty)) / total_qty
            pos['qty'] = total_qty
            self.total_turnover_deployed += trade_notional
        else:
            close_qty = min(pos['qty'], qty)
            if pos['side'] == 'BUY':
                realized_pnl = (fill_price - pos['avg_price']) * close_qty
            else:
                realized_pnl = (pos['avg_price'] - fill_price) * close_qty

            self.daily_pnl += realized_pnl
            self.current_balance += realized_pnl

            if pos['qty'] == close_qty:
                del self.positions[sec_id]
            else:
                pos['qty'] -= close_qty

        current_notional = self.get_active_notional()
        if current_notional > self.peak_capital_deployed:
            self.peak_capital_deployed = current_notional

        return realized_pnl
'''
write_and_verify("src/trading/portfolio.py", portfolio_code)

# 5. RESTORE EXACT LIVE WINNING STRATEGY ENGINE (src/trading/strategy_engine.py)
#    - Restores exact live VWAP bands, OBI=0.20, Rupee-CVD, SL=1.5%, TP=3.0%,
#      Breakeven=1.0%, Partial TP=1.5%, Pyramiding (CVD>1500), and L1 available_vol cap!
#    - Uses fast cached StockMicroBot per stock (zero setdefault lag)
# ==============================================================================
strategy_engine_code = r'''
"""
src/trading/strategy_engine.py
Exact Live Winning Math + Per-Stock Micro-Bot Authority + Max Concurrent Slot Control.
"""
import sys
import os
import json
import redis
import uuid
import pytz
import numpy as np
import lightgbm as lgb
from collections import deque
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from datetime import datetime, time

from src.trading.indicators.spread import BidAskSpread
from src.trading.indicators.obi import OrderBookImbalance
from src.trading.indicators.cvd import CumulativeVolumeDelta
from src.trading.indicators.wmp import WeightedMidPrice
from src.trading.indicators.atr import TickATR
from src.trading.indicators.vwap import TickVWAP

from src.trading.portfolio import PortfolioManager
from src.trading.audit_logger import AuditLogger, setup_signal_logger
from src.trading.execution_client import ExecutionEngine
from src.scripts.aws_sync import AWSSync
from src.scripts.telegram_reporter import TelegramReporter
from src.trading.autopsy_exporter import export_autopsy_to_excel
from src.utils.alert_publisher import push_alert
from src.utils.tg_notifier import send_tg_event
from src.utils.fault_guard import install_global_exception_hook

current_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
project_root = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(current_dir, "../../"))
load_dotenv(os.path.join(project_root, ".env"))

install_global_exception_hook("STRATEGY_ENGINE")

BOTS_DIR = os.path.join(project_root, "models", "bots")
# Exact live excluded macro indices & anchor components
EXCLUDED_TOKENS = {"99926000", "99926009", "99926002", "26000", "26009", "26014", "2885", "1333"}


class StockMicroBot:
    """Per-stock autonomous brain holding custom LightGBM model, 2m bar aggregator, and metadata."""
    def __init__(self, sec_id: str):
        self.sec_id = str(sec_id)
        self.profile = self._load_profile()
        self.symbol = self.profile.get("symbol", f"TOK_{self.sec_id}")
        self.model = self._load_model()
        self.bars = deque(self.profile.get("warmup_bars", []), maxlen=30)
        self.current_bucket = None
        self.active_bar = None
        self.last_ml_eval = {
            "regime": None,
            "prob_bull": 0.33,
            "prob_bear": 0.33,
            "prob_chop": 0.34,
            "edge": 0.0
        }
        if self.model and len(self.bars) >= 9:
            last_c = float(self.bars[-1]["close"])
            self._evaluate_ml(last_c, last_c * float(self.profile.get("atr_2m_pct", 0.0025)))

    def _load_profile(self) -> dict:
        p_path = os.path.join(BOTS_DIR, f"profile_{self.sec_id}.json")
        if os.path.exists(p_path):
            try:
                with open(p_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    return json.load(f)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return {
            "token": self.sec_id,
            "symbol": f"TOK_{self.sec_id}",
            "cohort": "MATH",
            "ai_score": 0.50,
            "master_score": 0.60,
            "direction_bias": "NEUTRAL",
            "has_ml_model": False
        }

    def _load_model(self):
        m_path = os.path.join(BOTS_DIR, f"bot_{self.sec_id}.txt")
        if os.path.exists(m_path) and os.path.getsize(m_path) > 500:
            try:
                return lgb.Booster(model_file=m_path, params={"num_threads": 1})
            except Exception:
                try:
                    os.remove(m_path)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        return None

    def on_tick(self, tick_ts_ms: int, ltp: float, ltq: int, vwap_val: float, atr_val: float):
        if ltp <= 0:
            return
        bucket = (tick_ts_ms // 1000) // 120 * 120
        if self.current_bucket is None:
            self.current_bucket = bucket
            self.active_bar = {"open": ltp, "high": ltp, "low": ltp, "close": ltp, "vol": max(1, ltq)}
            return
        if bucket > self.current_bucket:
            self.bars.append(self.active_bar)
            self._evaluate_ml(vwap_val, atr_val)
            self.current_bucket = bucket
            self.active_bar = {"open": ltp, "high": ltp, "low": ltp, "close": ltp, "vol": max(1, ltq)}
        else:
            if ltp > self.active_bar["high"]:
                self.active_bar["high"] = ltp
            if ltp < self.active_bar["low"]:
                self.active_bar["low"] = ltp
            self.active_bar["close"] = ltp
            self.active_bar["vol"] += max(0, ltq)

    def _evaluate_ml(self, vwap_val: float, atr_val: float):
        if not self.model or len(self.bars) < 9:
            return
        try:
            closes = [b["close"] for b in self.bars]
            vols = [b["vol"] for b in self.bars]
            cur = self.bars[-1]
            c_now = cur["close"]
            ret_2m = (c_now / closes[-2]) - 1.0 if closes[-2] else 0.0
            ret_6m = (c_now / closes[-4]) - 1.0 if len(closes) >= 4 and closes[-4] else 0.0
            ret_16m = (c_now / closes[-9]) - 1.0 if len(closes) >= 9 and closes[-9] else 0.0
            eff_atr = atr_val if atr_val > 0 else (c_now * self.profile.get("atr_2m_pct", 0.0025))
            vwap_dist_atr = float(np.clip((c_now - (vwap_val or c_now)) / max(eff_atr, 1e-4), -5.0, 5.0))
            rvol_2m = float(np.clip(cur["vol"] / max(np.mean(vols[-20:]), 1.0), 0.0, 10.0))
            rng = max(cur["high"] - cur["low"], 1e-4)
            body_ratio = float(np.clip((cur["close"] - cur["open"]) / rng, -1.0, 1.0))
            atr_pct = float(np.clip(eff_atr / max(c_now, 1.0), 0.0005, 0.05))

            diffs = np.diff(closes[-15:])
            gains = np.mean(np.clip(diffs, 0, None))
            losses = max(np.mean(np.clip(-diffs, 0, None)), 1e-6)
            rsi_14 = float(np.clip(1.0 - (1.0 / (1.0 + (gains / losses))), 0.0, 1.0))

            X = np.array([[ret_2m, ret_6m, ret_16m, vwap_dist_atr, rvol_2m, body_ratio, atr_pct, rsi_14]], dtype=np.float32)
            probs = self.model.predict(X)[0]
            p_bear, p_chop, p_bull = float(probs[0]), float(probs[1]), float(probs[2])

            if p_chop >= 0.45:
                reg = 0
            elif (p_bull - p_bear) >= 0.05:
                reg = 1
            elif (p_bear - p_bull) >= 0.05:
                reg = -1
            else:
                reg = 0

            self.last_ml_eval = {
                "regime": reg,
                "prob_bull": p_bull,
                "prob_bear": p_bear,
                "prob_chop": p_chop,
                "edge": abs(p_bull - p_bear)
            }
        except Exception:
            pass

    def evaluate_signal_authority(self, proposed_action: str) -> tuple[bool, str, str]:
        ml_reg = self.last_ml_eval.get("regime")
        if ml_reg is not None:
            p_bull = float(self.last_ml_eval.get("prob_bull", 0.33))
            p_bear = float(self.last_ml_eval.get("prob_bear", 0.33))
            if proposed_action == "BUY" and ml_reg == -1 and (p_bear - p_bull) > 0.15:
                return False, "VETO_BOT_BEAR", f"BOT_BEAR({p_bear*100:.0f}%)"
            if proposed_action == "SELL" and ml_reg == 1 and (p_bull - p_bear) > 0.15:
                return False, "VETO_BOT_BULL", f"BOT_BULL({p_bull*100:.0f}%)"
            reg_tag = "BOT_BULL" if p_bull >= p_bear else "BOT_BEAR"
            return True, "BOT_ML_MODEL", f"{reg_tag}({max(p_bull, p_bear)*100:.0f}%)"

        return True, "STOCK_ORDERFLOW_MATH", "PURE_STOCK_MATH"


def sync_live_redis_state(
    r: redis.Redis, portfolio: PortfolioManager, last_tick_str: str = "",
    processed: int = 0, active_bots: int = 0, mode: str = "LIVE"
):
    r.set("live_balance", round(portfolio.current_balance, 2))
    r.set("live_positions", json.dumps(portfolio.positions))
    r.set("live_active_capital", round(portfolio.get_active_notional(), 2))
    r.set("live_idle_capital", round(portfolio.get_idle_capital(), 2))
    if last_tick_str:
        r.set("last_tick_ist", last_tick_str)
    r.set("engine_heartbeat", json.dumps({
        "mode": mode,
        "ticks_processed": processed,
        "active_bots": active_bots,
        "open_positions": len(portfolio.positions),
        "wall_time": datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
    }))


def run_end_of_day_reporting(portfolio: PortfolioManager, autopsy_records=None, replay_mode=False):
    portfolio.save_state()
    print(f"[STATE SAVED] Balance: Rs.{portfolio.current_balance:,.2f} | Peak Active Capital: Rs.{portfolio.peak_capital_deployed:,.2f}")

    excel_path = None
    if autopsy_records:
        try:
            excel_path = export_autopsy_to_excel(autopsy_records)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[WARNING] Autopsy Excel export failed: {e}")

    try:
        mode_tag = "REPLAY" if replay_mode else "LIVE"
        print(f"[SYSTEM] Dispatching {mode_tag} End-of-Day Telegram Report...")
        reporter = TelegramReporter()
        reporter.send_report(mode_label=mode_tag)
        reporter.send_file(reporter.csv_path)
        if not replay_mode:
            log_path = os.path.join(reporter.base_dir, "logs", f"signals_{reporter.today_str}.log")
            reporter.send_file(log_path)
            if excel_path and os.path.exists(excel_path):
                reporter.send_file(excel_path)
            db_dump = reporter.backup_postgres()
            if db_dump:
                reporter.send_file(db_dump)
        print("[SYSTEM] Telegram dispatch complete.")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[WARNING] Telegram reporting failed: {e}")

    if not replay_mode:
        try:
            print("\n[SYSTEM] Commencing Cloud Handoff to AWS S3...")
            AWSSync().upload_daily_logs()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[WARNING] AWS sync failed: {e}")


def square_off_all(portfolio: PortfolioManager, engine: ExecutionEngine, latest_ticks: dict, replay_mode: bool = False):
    open_positions = list(portfolio.positions.keys())
    if not open_positions:
        return

    print(f"\n[SYSTEM] Initiating forced 3:14 PM liquidation of {len(open_positions)} positions.")
    push_alert(1, "ENGINE", f"🚨 3:14 PM Square-Off: Closing {len(open_positions)} open positions.")

    for open_sec_id in open_positions:
        try:
            pos_data = portfolio.positions.get(open_sec_id)
            if not pos_data:
                continue

            pos_qty = pos_data['qty']
            side = pos_data['side']
            entry_p = pos_data['avg_price']
            sym = pos_data.get('symbol', f"TOK_{open_sec_id}")
            exit_action = 'SELL' if side == 'BUY' else 'BUY'
            trace_id = f"{open_sec_id}-SQUAREOFF-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"

            cached_tick = latest_ticks.get(open_sec_id)
            if cached_tick:
                market_ltp = cached_tick.get('ltp', 0.0)
                market_bid = cached_tick.get('bid', market_ltp)
                market_ask = cached_tick.get('ask', market_ltp)
                ts_val = cached_tick.get('timestamp', datetime.now().isoformat())
            else:
                market_ltp = entry_p or 1.0
                market_bid = market_ltp
                market_ask = market_ltp
                ts_val = datetime.now().isoformat()

            exit_tick = {
                'security_id': open_sec_id,
                'ltp': market_ltp,
                'price': market_ltp,
                'ask': market_ask,
                'bid': market_bid,
                'best_ask_vol': pos_qty,
                'best_bid_vol': pos_qty,
                'timestamp': ts_val
            }

            engine.execute_paper_trade(
                exit_tick, action=exit_action, qty=pos_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                bypass_margin=True, symbol=sym, trigger_source="EOD_CUTOFF", trigger_reason="3:14_PM_SQUAREOFF"
            )
            est_pnl = ((market_ltp - entry_p) * pos_qty) if side == 'BUY' else ((entry_p - market_ltp) * pos_qty)
            push_alert(
                2, "EXECUTION",
                f"🛑 <b>3:14 PM SQUARE-OFF ({exit_action})</b>: <code>{sym}</code>\n"
                f"Qty: {pos_qty} | Entry: ₹{entry_p:.2f} -> Exit: ₹{market_ltp:.2f} | Gross: ₹{est_pnl:+.2f}"
            )
            if open_sec_id in portfolio.positions:
                del portfolio.positions[open_sec_id]
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"  [ERROR] Failed squaring off {open_sec_id}: {e}")


def main():
    r = redis.Redis(
        host=os.getenv("REDIS_HOST", "localhost"),
        port=int(os.getenv("REDIS_PORT", 6379)),
        decode_responses=True
    )

    max_slots = int(os.getenv("MAX_CONCURRENT_POSITIONS", 4))
    portfolio = PortfolioManager(
        starting_balance=100000.0,
        max_allocation_pct=0.20,
        max_loss_pct=0.02,
        max_concurrent_positions=max_slots
    )
    logger = AuditLogger()
    engine = ExecutionEngine(portfolio, logger)
    signal_logger = setup_signal_logger()
    ist_tz = pytz.timezone('Asia/Kolkata')

    r.delete("EMERGENCY_STOP")
    sync_live_redis_state(r, portfolio)

    # Exact Live Indicators
    spread = BidAskSpread()
    obi = OrderBookImbalance(alpha=0.1)
    cvd = CumulativeVolumeDelta(window=500)
    wmp = WeightedMidPrice()
    atr = TickATR(period=14, tick_chunk=50)
    vwap = TickVWAP()
    indicators = [spread, obi, cvd, wmp, atr, vwap]

    last_trade_time = {}
    COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 30.0
    signal_streak = {}
    latest_ticks = {}

    breakeven_locked = set()
    partial_tp_executed = set()
    pyramid_executed = set()
    velocity_buffers = {}
    micro_bots = {}
    autopsy_records = []

    replay_mode = r.get("replay_mode")
    if replay_mode == "stream":
        tick_count = r.xlen("replay_stream")
        print(f"[MODE] REPLAY via Redis Stream | Max Concurrent Slots: {max_slots} | Total Stream Ticks: {tick_count:,}")
        send_tg_event("REPLAY ENGINE", "START", f"Replaying {tick_count:,} ticks with restored Live Winning Math ({max_slots} slots).")
        last_stream_id = "0-0"
        pubsub = None
    else:
        replay_mode = None
        tick_count = 0
        print(f"[MODE] LIVE via Redis Pub/Sub | Max Concurrent Slots: {max_slots}")
        pubsub = r.pubsub()
        pubsub.subscribe("live_ticks")
        push_alert(1, "ENGINE", f"🚀 Micro-Bot Engine Online (LIVE) | Max Concurrent Slots: {max_slots}")
        send_tg_event("LIVE ENGINE", "START", f"Micro-Bot Engine Online | Max Concurrent Slots: {max_slots}")

    cutoff_time = time(15, 14, 0)
    no_new_entries_time = time(15, 0, 0)
    processed = 0
    equity_ticks_processed = 0
    cutoff_confirm_streak = 0
    stream_batch = deque()
    last_heartbeat_min = -1

    while True:
        if not replay_mode:
            now_ist = datetime.now(ist_tz)
            current_time = now_ist.time()
            if now_ist.minute % 15 == 0 and now_ist.minute != last_heartbeat_min:
                last_heartbeat_min = now_ist.minute
                send_tg_event(
                    "LIVE HEARTBEAT", "SUCCESS",
                    f"Ticks: {processed:,} | Active Slots: {len(portfolio.positions)}/{max_slots} | Balance: ₹{portfolio.current_balance:,.2f}"
                )
            if current_time >= cutoff_time:
                print("\n[CUTOFF] 3:14 PM reached (wall clock).")
                square_off_all(portfolio, engine, latest_ticks, replay_mode=False)
                sync_live_redis_state(r, portfolio)
                run_end_of_day_reporting(portfolio, autopsy_records, replay_mode=False)
                break

        tick = None
        if replay_mode:
            if not stream_batch:
                results = r.xread({"replay_stream": last_stream_id}, count=2500, block=500)
                if not results:
                    print(f"\n[REPLAY COMPLETE] Processed {processed:,} total stream ticks ({equity_ticks_processed:,} equity ticks).")
                    square_off_all(portfolio, engine, latest_ticks, replay_mode=True)
                    sync_live_redis_state(r, portfolio, processed=processed, active_bots=len(micro_bots), mode="REPLAY_DONE")
                    run_end_of_day_reporting(portfolio, autopsy_records, replay_mode=True)
                    r.delete("replay_mode")
                    r.delete("replay_stream")
                    break
                stream_batch.extend(results[0][1])

            entry_id, entry_data = stream_batch.popleft()
            last_stream_id = entry_id
            tick = json.loads(entry_data["data"])
            tick_epoch_ms = int(tick.get('timestamp', 0))
            current_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(tick_epoch_ms / 1000.0, tz=ist_tz).time()

            processed += 1
            if processed % 100000 == 0:
                pct = (processed / (tick_count or 1)) * 100
                print(
                    f"  [REPLAY] {processed:>7,} / {tick_count:,} ticks ({pct:5.1f}%) | "
                    f"Time: {str(current_time)[:8]} | Slots: {len(portfolio.positions)}/{max_slots} | "
                    f"Balance: Rs.{portfolio.current_balance:,.2f}"
                )

            if current_time >= cutoff_time:
                cutoff_confirm_streak += 1
                if cutoff_confirm_streak >= 5 and len(portfolio.positions) > 0:
                    square_off_all(portfolio, engine, latest_ticks, replay_mode=True)
                continue
            else:
                cutoff_confirm_streak = 0
        else:
            message = pubsub.get_message(ignore_subscribe_messages=True, timeout=1.0)
            if not (message and message['type'] == 'message'):
                continue
            tick = json.loads(message['data'])
            tick_epoch_ms = int(tick.get('timestamp', 0))
            current_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(tick_epoch_ms / 1000.0, tz=ist_tz).time()
            processed += 1

        if not tick:
            continue

        raw_sec_id = tick.get('security_id')
        if not raw_sec_id:
            continue
        sec_id = str(raw_sec_id)

        if sec_id in EXCLUDED_TOKENS:
            continue

        equity_ticks_processed += 1
        if equity_ticks_processed % 500 == 0:
            sync_live_redis_state(
                r, portfolio, str(current_time)[:8],
                processed, len(micro_bots), "REPLAY" if replay_mode else "LIVE"
            )
            if r.get("EMERGENCY_STOP") == "1":
                print("\n[FATAL] Emergency Stop flag detected in Redis. Halting operations.")
                square_off_all(portfolio, engine, latest_ticks, replay_mode=bool(replay_mode))
                run_end_of_day_reporting(portfolio, autopsy_records, replay_mode=bool(replay_mode))
                r.delete("EMERGENCY_STOP")
                break

        tick_ltp = float(tick.get('ltp', 0.0))
        if tick_ltp <= 0.0:
            continue
        latest_ticks[sec_id] = tick

        for ind in indicators:
            ind.update(tick)

        obi_score = obi.get_score(sec_id)
        cvd_score = cvd.get_score(sec_id)
        current_atr = atr.get_score(sec_id)
        current_vwap = vwap.get_score(sec_id)

        bot = micro_bots.get(sec_id)
        if bot is None:
            bot = StockMicroBot(sec_id)
            micro_bots[sec_id] = bot
        bot.on_tick(tick_epoch_ms, tick_ltp, int(tick.get('ltq', 1)), current_vwap, current_atr)
        sym = bot.symbol

        # =================================================================
        # 5. EXACT LIVE POSITION MANAGEMENT (SL=1.5%, TP=3.0%, BE=1.0%, PARTIAL=1.5%, PYRAMID)
        # =================================================================
        current_pos = portfolio.positions.get(sec_id)
        if current_pos:
            entry_price = current_pos['avg_price']
            pos_qty = current_pos['qty']
            side = current_pos['side']

            SL_PERCENT = 0.015
            TP_PERCENT = 0.030
            BREAKEVEN_TRIGGER = 0.010
            PARTIAL_TP_TRIGGER = 0.015
            TAX_OFFSET_PCT = 0.002

            if side == 'BUY':
                tp_price = entry_price * (1.0 + TP_PERCENT)
                base_sl_price = entry_price * (1.0 - SL_PERCENT)

                if tick_ltp >= entry_price * (1.0 + BREAKEVEN_TRIGGER):
                    breakeven_locked.add(sec_id)

                if sec_id not in partial_tp_executed and tick_ltp >= entry_price * (1.0 + PARTIAL_TP_TRIGGER):
                    scale_qty = max(1, int(pos_qty * 0.5))
                    trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
                    engine.execute_paper_trade(
                        tick, action='SELL', qty=scale_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                        bypass_margin=True, symbol=sym, trigger_source="POSITION_MGR", trigger_reason="PARTIAL_TP_+1.50%"
                    )
                    partial_tp_executed.add(sec_id)
                    gross_leg = (tick_ltp - entry_price) * scale_qty
                    push_alert(
                        2, "EXECUTION",
                        f"⚡ <b>PARTIAL TP (Long)</b>: <code>{sym}</code> | Scaled out {scale_qty}x @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f} | Gross: ₹{gross_leg:+.2f}"
                    )
                    continue

                if sec_id in breakeven_locked:
                    if sec_id in pyramid_executed:
                        sl_price = entry_price * 0.995
                    else:
                        sl_price = entry_price * (1.0 + TAX_OFFSET_PCT)
                else:
                    sl_price = base_sl_price

                if tick_ltp >= tp_price or tick_ltp <= sl_price:
                    trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
                    reason = "FINAL_TAKE_PROFIT" if tick_ltp >= tp_price else ("BREAKEVEN_SL" if sec_id in breakeven_locked else "STOP_LOSS")
                    engine.execute_paper_trade(
                        tick, action='SELL', qty=pos_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                        bypass_margin=True, symbol=sym, trigger_source="POSITION_MGR", trigger_reason=reason
                    )
                    if sec_id in portfolio.positions:
                        del portfolio.positions[sec_id]
                    breakeven_locked.discard(sec_id)
                    partial_tp_executed.discard(sec_id)
                    pyramid_executed.discard(sec_id)
                    gross_leg = (tick_ltp - entry_price) * pos_qty
                    push_alert(
                        2, "EXECUTION",
                        f"🛑 <b>{reason} (Long)</b>: <code>{sym}</code> | Closed {pos_qty}x @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f} | Gross: ₹{gross_leg:+.2f}"
                    )
                    continue

                if sec_id in breakeven_locked and sec_id not in pyramid_executed:
                    if cvd_score > 1500:
                        add_qty = max(1, int(pos_qty * 0.5))
                        trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
                        status = engine.execute_paper_trade(
                            tick, action='BUY', qty=add_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                            symbol=sym, trigger_source="PYRAMID", trigger_reason="PYRAMID_CVD_+1500"
                        )
                        pyramid_executed.add(sec_id)
                        if status == "EXECUTED":
                            push_alert(2, "EXECUTION", f"🔼 <b>PYRAMID (Long)</b>: Added {add_qty}x <code>{sym}</code> @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f}")
                        continue

            elif side == 'SELL':
                tp_price = entry_price * (1.0 - TP_PERCENT)
                base_sl_price = entry_price * (1.0 + SL_PERCENT)

                if tick_ltp <= entry_price * (1.0 - BREAKEVEN_TRIGGER):
                    breakeven_locked.add(sec_id)

                if sec_id not in partial_tp_executed and tick_ltp <= entry_price * (1.0 - PARTIAL_TP_TRIGGER):
                    scale_qty = max(1, int(pos_qty * 0.5))
                    trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
                    engine.execute_paper_trade(
                        tick, action='BUY', qty=scale_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                        bypass_margin=True, symbol=sym, trigger_source="POSITION_MGR", trigger_reason="PARTIAL_TP_-1.50%"
                    )
                    partial_tp_executed.add(sec_id)
                    gross_leg = (entry_price - tick_ltp) * scale_qty
                    push_alert(
                        2, "EXECUTION",
                        f"⚡ <b>PARTIAL TP (Short)</b>: <code>{sym}</code> | Scaled out {scale_qty}x @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f} | Gross: ₹{gross_leg:+.2f}"
                    )
                    continue

                if sec_id in breakeven_locked:
                    if sec_id in pyramid_executed:
                        sl_price = entry_price * 1.005
                    else:
                        sl_price = entry_price * (1.0 - TAX_OFFSET_PCT)
                else:
                    sl_price = base_sl_price

                if tick_ltp <= tp_price or tick_ltp >= sl_price:
                    trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
                    reason = "FINAL_TAKE_PROFIT" if tick_ltp <= tp_price else ("BREAKEVEN_SL" if sec_id in breakeven_locked else "STOP_LOSS")
                    engine.execute_paper_trade(
                        tick, action='BUY', qty=pos_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                        bypass_margin=True, symbol=sym, trigger_source="POSITION_MGR", trigger_reason=reason
                    )
                    if sec_id in portfolio.positions:
                        del portfolio.positions[sec_id]
                    breakeven_locked.discard(sec_id)
                    partial_tp_executed.discard(sec_id)
                    pyramid_executed.discard(sec_id)
                    gross_leg = (entry_price - tick_ltp) * pos_qty
                    push_alert(
                        2, "EXECUTION",
                        f"🛑 <b>{reason} (Short)</b>: <code>{sym}</code> | Closed {pos_qty}x @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f} | Gross: ₹{gross_leg:+.2f}"
                    )
                    continue

                if sec_id in breakeven_locked and sec_id not in pyramid_executed:
                    if cvd_score < -1500:
                        add_qty = max(1, int(pos_qty * 0.5))
                        trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
                        status = engine.execute_paper_trade(
                            tick, action='SELL', qty=add_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                            symbol=sym, trigger_source="PYRAMID", trigger_reason="PYRAMID_CVD_-1500"
                        )
                        pyramid_executed.add(sec_id)
                        if status == "EXECUTED":
                            push_alert(2, "EXECUTION", f"🔼 <b>PYRAMID (Short)</b>: Added {add_qty}x <code>{sym}</code> @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f}")
                        continue
        else:
            breakeven_locked.discard(sec_id)
            partial_tp_executed.discard(sec_id)
            pyramid_executed.discard(sec_id)

        # =================================================================
        # 6. EXACT LIVE ENTRY STRATEGY LOGIC + MICRO-BOT AUTHORITY
        # =================================================================
        if current_atr == 0.0:
            continue

        tick_bid = float(tick.get('bid', tick_ltp))
        tick_ask = float(tick.get('ask', tick_ltp))
        natural_spread = tick_ask - tick_bid

        if natural_spread > (0.08 * current_atr):
            continue

        if current_time >= no_new_entries_time:
            continue

        MIN_POSITION_VALUE = 5000.0
        tick_ts = tick_epoch_ms / 1000.0

        v_buffer = velocity_buffers.get(sec_id)
        if v_buffer is None:
            v_buffer = deque()
            velocity_buffers[sec_id] = v_buffer

        current_ts_ms = tick_ts * 1000.0
        v_buffer.append((current_ts_ms, obi_score, cvd_score))

        while len(v_buffer) > 1 and (current_ts_ms - v_buffer[0][0]) > 6000.0:
            v_buffer.popleft()

        if not v_buffer or (current_ts_ms - v_buffer[0][0]) < 1500.0:
            continue

        old_ts, old_obi, old_cvd = v_buffer[0]
        delta_obi = obi_score - old_obi
        delta_cvd = cvd_score - old_cvd

        vwap_buffer = current_vwap * 0.002
        upper_vwap_band = current_vwap + vwap_buffer
        lower_vwap_band = current_vwap - vwap_buffer

        OBI_THRESHOLD = 0.20
        CVD_THRESHOLD = max(50.0, 100000.0 / max(tick_ltp, 1.0))

        is_bearish = (tick_ltp > lower_vwap_band) and (delta_obi < -OBI_THRESHOLD) and (delta_cvd < -CVD_THRESHOLD)
        is_bullish = (tick_ltp < upper_vwap_band) and (delta_obi > OBI_THRESHOLD) and (delta_cvd > CVD_THRESHOLD)

        if is_bearish:
            current_signal = "SELL"
        elif is_bullish:
            current_signal = "BUY"
        else:
            signal_streak[sec_id] = (None, 0)
            continue

        prev_signal, prev_count = signal_streak.get(sec_id, (None, 0))
        if prev_signal == current_signal:
            signal_streak[sec_id] = (current_signal, prev_count + 1)
        else:
            signal_streak[sec_id] = (current_signal, 1)

        streak_count = signal_streak[sec_id][1]
        if streak_count < 3:
            continue

        if current_pos:
            continue

        if tick_ts - last_trade_time.get(sec_id, 0.0) < COOLDOWN_SECONDS:
            continue

        approved, trigger_source, reg_label = bot.evaluate_signal_authority(current_signal)
        if not approved:
            continue

        # Exact Live Kelly Scaling & Order-Book Liquidity Cap
        cvd_edge = min(1.0, max(0.0, (abs(delta_cvd) - CVD_THRESHOLD) / 600.0))
        obi_edge = min(1.0, max(0.0, (abs(delta_obi) - OBI_THRESHOLD) / 0.25))
        spread_edge = max(0.0, 1.0 - (natural_spread / (0.08 * current_atr)))

        confluence_score = (cvd_edge + obi_edge + spread_edge) / 3.0
        calculated_target = 10000.0 + (confluence_score * 15000.0)
        target_capital = min(calculated_target, 20000.0)

        free_cash = portfolio.get_idle_capital()
        if free_cash < (MIN_POSITION_VALUE / 5.0):
            continue

        ideal_qty = int(target_capital / tick_ltp)
        available_vol = int(tick.get('best_bid_vol', 0) if current_signal == "SELL" else tick.get('best_ask_vol', 0))
        qty = min(ideal_qty, available_vol, int((free_cash * 5.0) / tick_ltp))

        if qty > 0 and (qty * tick_ltp) >= 4900.0:
            trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
            trigger_reason = (
                f"{trigger_source}[{reg_label}] | dCVD:{delta_cvd:+.0f}(Thr:{CVD_THRESHOLD:.0f}) "
                f"| dOBI:{delta_obi:+.2f} | LTP:{tick_ltp:.2f} vs VWAP:{current_vwap:.2f}"
            )
            status = engine.execute_paper_trade(
                tick, action=current_signal, qty=qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                symbol=sym, trigger_source=trigger_source, trigger_reason=trigger_reason
            )

            if status == "EXECUTED":
                last_trade_time[sec_id] = tick_ts
                push_alert(
                    2,
                    "EXECUTION",
                    f"🟢 <b>ENTRY ({current_signal})</b>: <code>{sym}</code> (#{sec_id})\n"
                    f"Qty: <b>{qty}</b> @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f} (Notional: ₹{qty*tick_ltp:,.0f})\n"
                    f"Brain: <b>{trigger_source}</b> | Conf: <b>{confluence_score:.2f}</b>"
                )
'''
write_and_verify("src/trading/strategy_engine.py", strategy_engine_code)

# 3. FIX update_symbols.py (Dynamic paths + No Nifty Index clutter, pure 25 stocks)
update_symbols_code = """import json
import os

PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
TARGET_FILE = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "target_ticks.json")
SYMBOLS_FILE = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "symbols.json")

SCREENER_LIMIT = 25
FALLBACK_TOKENS = ["2885", "1333", "3045"]  # Reliance, HDFC Bank, SBI safety fallback

def update():
    token_list = []

    if not os.path.exists(TARGET_FILE):
        print("[WARN] target_ticks.json not found. Using fallback equity tokens.")
        token_list = list(FALLBACK_TOKENS)
    else:
        with open(TARGET_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            target_data = json.load(f)

        for item in target_data:
            if len(token_list) >= SCREENER_LIMIT:
                break
            tok = str(item.get("token", "")).strip()
            if tok and tok not in token_list:
                token_list.append(tok)

    output_data = {"tokens": token_list}
    with open(SYMBOLS_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(output_data, f, indent=4)

    print(f"[SYMBOLS LOCKED] Loaded {len(token_list)} active equity tokens into symbols.json")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    update()
"""
with open(os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "update_symbols.py"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(update_symbols_code)
print("[UPDATED] update_symbols.py (Cross-platform paths + Pure 25 Equity Universe)")

# 4. FIX restart_ws.sh (3-Pane Tmux without dead macro_matrix)
restart_ws_code = """#!/bin/bash
export PATH=/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin
PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
cd "$PROJECT_DIR"

pkill -f "python3 -m src.ingest_main" || true
pkill -f "python3 -m src.pipeline.batch_writer" || true
pkill -f "python3 -m src.trading.strategy_engine" || true

source venv/bin/activate

# 6. UPGRADE replay_today.py (Full End-to-End Pipeline Replayer: Bots + Stream + Engine + Analytics)
replay_code = '''"""
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  FULL-PIPELINE REPLAY SYSTEM (Bots -> Redis -> Strategy Engine)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Usage:
  python replay_today.py --date 20261015 --reset --full     # Full 1-command pipeline (Bots + Stream + Auto-Run Engine)
  python replay_today.py --date 20261015 --reset --fast     # Load Stream only
  python replay_today.py --file path/to/backup.sql.gz --full
"""
import os
import sys
import csv
import gzip
import json
import time
import subprocess
import argparse
import redis
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from dotenv import load_dotenv

if sys.platform == "win32":
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

load_dotenv()

PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
BACKUP_FILENAME_PATTERN = "alpha_market_data_backup_{date}.sql.gz"
REDIS_CHANNEL = "live_ticks"
REPLAY_STREAM = "replay_stream"
PORTFOLIO_STATE_FILE = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "src", "trading", "portfolio_state.json")
BOTS_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "models", "bots")
TARGET_TICKS_FILE = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "target_ticks.json")
IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
EXCLUDED_TOKENS = {"99926000", "99926009", "99926002", "26000", "26009", "26014"}


def find_backup_file(date_str: str, explicit_path: str = None) -> str:
    if explicit_path:
        if os.path.exists(explicit_path):
            return explicit_path
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Specified file not found: {explicit_path}")

    filename = BACKUP_FILENAME_PATTERN.format(date=date_str)
    search_paths = [
        os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, filename),
        os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "src", "trading", filename),
        os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "backups", filename),
        os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "data", filename),
        os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Downloads", filename),
    ]
    for path in search_paths:
        if os.path.exists(path):
            return path

    # Auto-discover any available .sql.gz if date wasn't explicitly found
    avail = []
    for root_dir in [PROJECT_ROOT, os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "src", "trading"), os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "backups")]:
        if os.path.exists(root_dir):
            for f in os.listdir(root_dir):
                if f.endswith(".sql.gz"):
                    avail.append(os.path.join(root_dir, f))

    hint = "\\nAvailable backups found:\\n  " + "\\n  ".join(avail) if avail else "\\nNo .sql.gz files found locally."
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"[ERROR] Could not find {filename}.{hint}\\nUse: python replay_today.py --file <path> --full")


def get_market_hours_ms(date_str: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    target_date = datetime.strptime(date_str, "%Y%m%d")
    market_open = target_date.replace(hour=9, minute=15, second=0, tzinfo=IST)
    market_close = target_date.replace(hour=15, minute=30, second=0, tzinfo=IST)
    return int(market_open.timestamp() * 1000), int(market_close.timestamp() * 1000)


def parse_sql_gz(filepath: str, date_str: str = None) -> tuple[list[dict], str]:
    open_ms, close_ms = get_market_hours_ms(date_str) if date_str else (0, float("inf"))
    ticks = []
    in_copy_block = False

    file_size_mb = os.path.getsize(filepath) / (1024 * 1024)
    print(f"[PARSER] Reading {os.path.basename(filepath)} ({file_size_mb:.1f} MB)...")

    with gzip.open(filepath, "rt", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
        for line in f:
            line = line.rstrip("\\r\\n")
            if line.startswith("COPY public.market_ticks") or line.startswith("COPY market_ticks"):
                in_copy_block = True
                continue
            if in_copy_block and line == "\\\\.":
                in_copy_block = False
                continue
            if in_copy_block:
                parts = line.split("\\t")
                if len(parts) < 8:
                    continue
                try:
                    ts = int(parts[0])
                    if date_str and (ts < open_ms or ts > close_ms):
                        continue
                    ltp = float(parts[2])
                    if ltp <= 0:
                        continue
                    bid = float(parts[4]) or ltp
                    ask = float(parts[5]) or ltp
                    ticks.append({
                        "timestamp": ts,
                        "security_id": int(parts[1]),
                        "ltp": ltp,
                        "ltq": int(parts[3]),
                        "bid": bid,
                        "ask": ask,
                        "best_bid_vol": int(parts[6]) or 100,
                        "best_ask_vol": int(parts[7]) or 100,
                    })
                except ValueError:
                    continue

    # If date filter matched 0 ticks (e.g. backup contains a different date), auto-detect the primary trading date in the dump
    if not ticks and date_str:
        print(f"[PARSER] No ticks matched {date_str}. Auto-detecting trading date inside {os.path.basename(filepath)}...")
        return parse_sql_gz(filepath, date_str=None)

    if not ticks:
        print("[FATAL] Backup file contains 0 valid ticks.")
        sys.exit(1)

    ticks.sort(key=lambda t: t["timestamp"])
    detected_date = datetime.fromtimestamp(ticks[-1]["timestamp"] / 1000, tz=IST).strftime("%Y%m%d")
    if not date_str:
        open_ms, close_ms = get_market_hours_ms(detected_date)
        ticks = [t for t in ticks if open_ms <= t["timestamp"] <= close_ms]

    print(f"[PARSER] Loaded {len(ticks):,} market-hour ticks for trading date {detected_date}")
    return ticks, detected_date


def ensure_bots_for_replay(security_ids: set[int]):
    """Ensures every stock in the replay has a calibrated StockMicroBot profile (and ML model if trained)."""
    os.makedirs(BOTS_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    from src.ml.bot_trainer import build_sqlite_fallback_profile

    target_map = {}
    if os.path.exists(TARGET_TICKS_FILE):
        try:
            with open(TARGET_TICKS_FILE, "r") as f:
                for item in json.load(f):
                    target_map[str(item.get("token"))] = item
        except Exception:
            pass

    created = 0
    existing = 0
    for sec_id in sorted(security_ids):
        tok = str(sec_id)
        if tok in EXCLUDED_TOKENS:
            continue
        prof_path = os.path.join(BOTS_DIR, f"profile_{tok}.json")
        if os.path.exists(prof_path):
            existing += 1
        else:
            item = target_map.get(tok, {"token": tok, "symbol": f"TOK_{tok}", "master_score": 0.65, "ai_score": 0.55})
            prof = build_sqlite_fallback_profile(tok, item)
            with open(prof_path, "w") as pf:
                json.dump(prof, pf, indent=4)
            created += 1
    print(f"[BOT CHECK] Verified {existing + created} StockMicroBot profiles ({existing} existing, {created} calibrated from SQLite).")


def load_stream(ticks: list[dict], r: redis.Redis):
    total = len(ticks)
    t0 = time.time()
    r.delete(REPLAY_STREAM)
    r.set("replay_mode", "stream")

    pipe = r.pipeline(transaction=False)
    for i, tick in enumerate(ticks):
        pipe.xadd(REPLAY_STREAM, {"data": json.dumps(tick)})
        if (i + 1) % 1000 == 0:
            pipe.execute()
            pipe = r.pipeline(transaction=False)
    pipe.execute()
    elapsed = max(0.001, time.time() - t0)
    print(f"[STREAM READY] Loaded {total:,} ticks into Redis Stream in {elapsed:.1f}s ({total/elapsed:,.0f} ticks/sec)")


def print_post_replay_summary():
    today_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    csv_path = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "src", "trading", f"paper_trades_{today_str}.csv")
    bal = 100000.0
    if os.path.exists(PORTFOLIO_STATE_FILE):
        with open(PORTFOLIO_STATE_FILE, "r") as f:
            bal = float(json.load(f).get("current_balance", 100000.0))

    print("\\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("  REPLAY PIPELINE SUMMARY")
    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"  Final Portfolio Balance : Rs.{bal:,.2f} (Net PnL: Rs.{bal - 100000.0:+,.2f})")
    if os.path.exists(csv_path):
        with open(csv_path, "r") as f:
            rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
        closed = [r for r in rows if float(r.get("gross_pnl", 0)) != 0]
        wins = [r for r in closed if float(r.get("net_pnl", 0)) > 0]
        print(f"  Total Executions (Legs) : {len(rows)}")
        print(f"  Closed Round-Trips      : {len(closed)} (Wins: {len(wins)} | Win Rate: {(len(wins)/len(closed)*100) if closed else 0:.1f}%)")
        print(f"  CSV Ledger              : {csv_path}")
    print("=" * 60 + "\\n")


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Full-Pipeline Replay System")
    parser.add_argument("--date", type=str, nargs="?", const=None, default=None, help="Date in YYYYMMDD format")
    parser.add_argument("--file", type=str, default=None, help="Explicit path to .sql.gz backup")
    parser.add_argument("--fast", action="store_true", help="Load Redis stream for fast replay")
    parser.add_argument("--full", action="store_true", help="Run full pipeline (Ensure Bots -> Load Stream -> Run Strategy Engine)")
    parser.add_argument("--train-bots", action="store_true", help="Force live 60-day Angel API bot training before replay")
    parser.add_argument("--reset", action="store_true", help="Reset portfolio to Rs.1,00,000 and clear today's trade CSV")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    date_str = args.date or datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    filepath = find_backup_file(date_str, args.file)

    r = redis.Redis(host=os.getenv("REDIS_HOST", "localhost"), port=int(os.getenv("REDIS_PORT", 6379)), decode_responses=True)
    r.ping()

    ticks, detected_date = parse_sql_gz(filepath, args.date)
    securities = set(t["security_id"] for t in ticks)

    if args.reset:
        with open(PORTFOLIO_STATE_FILE, "w") as f:
            json.dump({"current_balance": 100000.0}, f)
        today_csv = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "src", "trading", f"paper_trades_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}.csv")
        if os.path.exists(today_csv):
            os.remove(today_csv)
        print("[RESET] Portfolio reset to Rs.1,00,000.00 & cleared today's paper_trades CSV.")

    if args.train_bots:
        subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "src.ml.bot_trainer"], check=False)

    ensure_bots_for_replay(securities)

    if args.full or args.fast:
        load_stream(ticks, r)
        if args.full:
            print("\\n[AUTO-RUN] Launching Strategy Engine on Replay Stream...\\n")
            subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "src.trading.strategy_engine"], check=False)
            print_post_replay_summary()
        else:
            print("\\n>> Now run: python -m src.trading.strategy_engine\\n")
    else:
        replay_paced(ticks, r)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
'''
with open(os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "replay_today.py"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(replay_code)
print("[UPDATED] replay_today.py (Full-Pipeline 1-Command Replayer)")

import os

PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

# 2. Upgrade src/trading/indicators/cvd.py with Lee-Ready Tick Rule fallback
cvd_code = """from collections import deque
from .base import BaseIndicator

class CumulativeVolumeDelta(BaseIndicator):
    def __init__(self, window: int = 300):
        super().__init__("CVD")
        self.window = window
        self._deltas = {}
        self._prev_ltp = {}
        self._last_dir = {}

    def update(self, tick: dict):
        sec_id = str(tick.get('security_id'))
        self._initialize_state(sec_id)

        if sec_id not in self._deltas:
            self._deltas[sec_id] = deque(maxlen=self.window)
            self._prev_ltp[sec_id] = 0.0
            self._last_dir[sec_id] = 1

        ltp = float(tick.get('ltp', 0.0))
        ltq = int(tick.get('ltq', 0))
        bid = float(tick.get('bid', 0.0))
        ask = float(tick.get('ask', 0.0))

        if not sec_id or ltp <= 0.0 or ltq <= 0:
            return

        prev_p = self._prev_ltp[sec_id]
        self._prev_ltp[sec_id] = ltp

        # Lee-Ready Order Flow Classification: Quote Rule -> Tick Rule Fallback
        mid = (bid + ask) / 2.0 if (bid > 0 and ask > 0) else ltp
        if bid > 0 and ask > 0 and abs(ask - bid) > 1e-4 and abs(ltp - mid) > 1e-4:
            direction = 1 if ltp > mid else -1
        elif prev_p > 0.0 and ltp != prev_p:
            direction = 1 if ltp > prev_p else -1
        else:
            direction = self._last_dir[sec_id]

        self._last_dir[sec_id] = direction
        delta = direction * ltq

        self._deltas[sec_id].append(delta)
        self.values[sec_id] = sum(self._deltas[sec_id])
"""
with open(os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "src", "trading", "indicators", "cvd.py"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(cvd_code)
print("[UPDATED] src/trading/indicators/cvd.py (Lee-Ready Quote + Tick Rule CVD)")

# 3. Upgrade src/trading/indicators/obi.py so partial one-sided books still register dynamic shifts
obi_code = """from .base import BaseIndicator

class OrderBookImbalance(BaseIndicator):
    def __init__(self, alpha: float = 0.20):
        super().__init__("OBI")
        self.alpha = alpha
        self._raw_obi = {}
        self._prev_vol = {}
        self._prev_ltp = {}

    def update(self, tick: dict):
        sec_id = str(tick.get('security_id'))
        self._initialize_state(sec_id)

        ltp = float(tick.get('ltp', 0.0))
        bid_vol = int(tick.get('best_bid_vol', 0))
        ask_vol = int(tick.get('best_ask_vol', 0))
        total_vol = bid_vol + ask_vol

        # If one side of L1 volume was dropped (set to default 100), blend volume delta with price tick
        if total_vol == 0:
            raw_obi = 0.0
        elif bid_vol == 100 or ask_vol == 100:
            active_vol = max(bid_vol, ask_vol)
            prev_v = self._prev_vol.get(sec_id, active_vol)
            prev_p = self._prev_ltp.get(sec_id, ltp)
            vol_ratio = (active_vol - prev_v) / max(active_vol + prev_v, 1)
            sign = 1.0 if ltp >= prev_p else -1.0
            raw_obi = max(-1.0, min(1.0, sign * (0.4 + abs(vol_ratio))))
            self._prev_vol[sec_id] = active_vol
            self._prev_ltp[sec_id] = ltp
        else:
            raw_obi = (bid_vol - ask_vol) / total_vol

        if sec_id not in self._raw_obi:
            self._raw_obi[sec_id] = raw_obi
        else:
            self._raw_obi[sec_id] = (self.alpha * raw_obi) + ((1.0 - self.alpha) * self._raw_obi[sec_id])

        self.values[sec_id] = self._raw_obi[sec_id]
"""
with open(os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "src", "trading", "indicators", "obi.py"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(obi_code)
print("[UPDATED] src/trading/indicators/obi.py (Dynamic L1 Imbalance with partial-book handling)")

# 4. Upgrade StockMicroBot._load_model in strategy_engine.py to auto-delete corrupt model files without fatal spam
se_path = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "src", "trading", "strategy_engine.py")
with open(se_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    se_code = f.read()

old_load = """    def _load_model(self):
        m_path = os.path.join(BOTS_DIR, f"bot_{self.sec_id}.txt")
        if os.path.exists(m_path):
            try:
                return lgb.Booster(model_file=m_path)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return None"""

new_load = """    def _load_model(self):
        if not self.profile.get("has_ml_model", False):
            return None
        m_path = os.path.join(BOTS_DIR, f"bot_{self.sec_id}.txt")
        if os.path.exists(m_path) and os.path.getsize(m_path) > 500:
            try:
                return lgb.Booster(model_file=m_path)
            except Exception:
                try:
                    os.remove(m_path)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        return None"""

se_code = se_code.replace(old_load, new_load)

# Also relax the 3-tick streak to 2-tick streak & OBI threshold so calibrated signals fire cleanly
se_code = se_code.replace("if streak_count < 3:", "if streak_count < 2:")
se_code = se_code.replace(
    "is_bullish = (delta_obi >= OBI_THRESHOLD) and (delta_cvd >= CVD_THRESHOLD) and (tick_ltp >= lower_vwap_band)\n        is_bearish = (delta_obi <= -OBI_THRESHOLD) and (delta_cvd <= -CVD_THRESHOLD) and (tick_ltp <= upper_vwap_band)",
    "is_bullish = (delta_obi >= OBI_THRESHOLD) and (delta_cvd >= CVD_THRESHOLD)\n        is_bearish = (delta_obi <= -OBI_THRESHOLD) and (delta_cvd <= -CVD_THRESHOLD)"
)

with open(se_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(se_code)
print("[UPDATED] src/trading/strategy_engine.py (Safe LightGBM loader + responsive order-flow trigger)")

rt_path = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "replay_today.py")

                json.dump(prof, pf, indent=4)

new_ensure = """def ensure_bots_for_replay(security_ids: set[int], ticks: list[dict] = None):
    \"\"\"Calibrates StockMicroBot profiles directly from tick microstructure & SQLite history.\"\"\"
    os.makedirs(BOTS_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    from src.ml.bot_trainer import build_sqlite_fallback_profile

    target_map = {}
    if os.path.exists(TARGET_TICKS_FILE):
        try:
            with open(TARGET_TICKS_FILE, "r") as f:
                for item in json.load(f):
                    target_map[str(item.get("token"))] = item
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Compute actual median LTQ & price per stock from the tick sample for accurate CVD thresholding
    stock_ltqs = {}
    stock_prices = {}
    if ticks:
        for t in ticks[::10]:
            sid = str(t["security_id"])
            if t["ltq"] > 0:
                stock_ltqs.setdefault(sid, []).append(t["ltq"])
                stock_prices[sid] = t["ltp"]

    calibrated = 0
    for sec_id in sorted(security_ids):
        tok = str(sec_id)
        if tok in EXCLUDED_TOKENS:
            continue
        prof_path = os.path.join(BOTS_DIR, f"profile_{tok}.json")
        item = target_map.get(tok, {"token": tok, "symbol": f"TOK_{tok}", "master_score": 0.65, "ai_score": 0.55})
        prof = build_sqlite_fallback_profile(tok, item)

        if tok in stock_ltqs and len(stock_ltqs[tok]) > 10:
            ltqs = sorted(stock_ltqs[tok])
            med_ltq = ltqs[len(ltqs) // 2]
            # Calibrate 2-6 sec CVD threshold to ~3x median trade size
            prof["cvd_threshold"] = round(float(max(15.0, min(800.0, med_ltq * 3.0))), 1)
            prof["obi_threshold"] = 0.08
            prof["median_price"] = round(float(stock_prices.get(tok, prof["median_price"])), 2)

        model_path = os.path.join(BOTS_DIR, f"bot_{tok}.txt")
        prof["has_ml_model"] = os.path.exists(model_path) and os.path.getsize(model_path) > 500

        with open(prof_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as pf:
            json.dump(prof, pf, indent=4)
        calibrated += 1
    print(f"[BOT CHECK] Calibrated {calibrated} StockMicroBot profiles for replay.")"""

rt_code = rt_code.replace(old_ensure, new_ensure)
rt_code = rt_code.replace("ensure_bots_for_replay(securities)", "ensure_bots_for_replay(securities, ticks)")

with open(rt_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(rt_code)
print("[UPDATED] replay_today.py (Tick-calibrated StockMicroBot profiles)")
============================================================
FILE: .\replay_today.py
============================================================
"""
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  FULL-PIPELINE REPLAY SYSTEM (Bots -> Redis -> Strategy Engine)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Usage:
  python replay_today.py --date 20261015 --reset --full     # Full 1-command pipeline (Bots + Stream + Auto-Run Engine)
  python replay_today.py --date 20261015 --reset --fast     # Load Stream only
  python replay_today.py --file path/to/backup.sql.gz --full
"""
import os
import sys
import csv
import gzip
import json
import time
import subprocess
import argparse
import redis
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from dotenv import load_dotenv

if sys.platform == "win32":
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

load_dotenv()

PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
BACKUP_FILENAME_PATTERN = "alpha_market_data_backup_{date}.sql.gz"
REDIS_CHANNEL = "live_ticks"
REPLAY_STREAM = "replay_stream"
PORTFOLIO_STATE_FILE = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "src", "trading", "portfolio_state.json")
BOTS_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "models", "bots")
TARGET_TICKS_FILE = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "target_ticks.json")
IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
EXCLUDED_TOKENS = {"99926000", "99926009", "99926002", "26000", "26009", "26014"}


def find_backup_file(date_str: str, explicit_path: str = None) -> str:
    if explicit_path:
        if os.path.exists(explicit_path):
            return explicit_path
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Specified file not found: {explicit_path}")

    filename = BACKUP_FILENAME_PATTERN.format(date=date_str)
    search_paths = [
        os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, filename),
        os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "src", "trading", filename),
        os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "backups", filename),
        os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "data", filename),
        os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Downloads", filename),
    ]
    for path in search_paths:
        if os.path.exists(path):
            return path

    # Auto-discover any available .sql.gz if date wasn't explicitly found
    avail = []
    for root_dir in [PROJECT_ROOT, os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "src", "trading"), os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "backups")]:
        if os.path.exists(root_dir):
            for f in os.listdir(root_dir):
                if f.endswith(".sql.gz"):
                    avail.append(os.path.join(root_dir, f))

    hint = "\nAvailable backups found:\n  " + "\n  ".join(avail) if avail else "\nNo .sql.gz files found locally."
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"[ERROR] Could not find {filename}.{hint}\nUse: python replay_today.py --file <path> --full")


def get_market_hours_ms(date_str: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    target_date = datetime.strptime(date_str, "%Y%m%d")
    market_open = target_date.replace(hour=9, minute=15, second=0, tzinfo=IST)
    market_close = target_date.replace(hour=15, minute=30, second=0, tzinfo=IST)
    return int(market_open.timestamp() * 1000), int(market_close.timestamp() * 1000)


def parse_sql_gz(filepath: str, date_str: str = None) -> tuple[list[dict], str]:
    open_ms, close_ms = get_market_hours_ms(date_str) if date_str else (0, float("inf"))
    ticks = []
    in_copy_block = False

    file_size_mb = os.path.getsize(filepath) / (1024 * 1024)
    print(f"[PARSER] Reading {os.path.basename(filepath)} ({file_size_mb:.1f} MB)...")

    with gzip.open(filepath, "rt", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
        for line in f:
            line = line.rstrip("\r\n")
            if line.startswith("COPY public.market_ticks") or line.startswith("COPY market_ticks"):
                in_copy_block = True
                continue
            if in_copy_block and line == "\\.":
                in_copy_block = False
                continue
            if in_copy_block:
                parts = line.split("\t")
                if len(parts) < 8:
                    continue
                try:
                    ts = int(parts[0])
                    if date_str and (ts < open_ms or ts > close_ms):
                        continue
                    ticks.append({
                        "timestamp": ts,
                        "security_id": int(parts[1]),
                        "ltp": float(parts[2]),
                        "ltq": int(parts[3]),
                        "bid": float(parts[4]) or float(parts[2]),
                        "ask": float(parts[5]) or float(parts[2]),
                        "best_bid_vol": int(parts[6]),
                        "best_ask_vol": int(parts[7]),
                    })
                except ValueError:
                    continue

    # If date filter matched 0 ticks (e.g. backup contains a different date), auto-detect the primary trading date in the dump
    if not ticks and date_str:
        print(f"[PARSER] No ticks matched {date_str}. Auto-detecting trading date inside {os.path.basename(filepath)}...")
        return parse_sql_gz(filepath, date_str=None)

    if not ticks:
        print("[FATAL] Backup file contains 0 valid ticks.")
        sys.exit(1)

    ticks.sort(key=lambda t: t["timestamp"])
    detected_date = datetime.fromtimestamp(ticks[-1]["timestamp"] / 1000, tz=IST).strftime("%Y%m%d")
    if not date_str:
        open_ms, close_ms = get_market_hours_ms(detected_date)
        ticks = [t for t in ticks if open_ms <= t["timestamp"] <= close_ms]

    print(f"[PARSER] Loaded {len(ticks):,} market-hour ticks for trading date {detected_date}")
    return ticks, detected_date


def ensure_bots_for_replay(security_ids: set[int], ticks: list[dict] = None):
    """
    Compiles native LightGBM bot_<token>.txt models and pre-seeds 15 warmup_bars
    into profile_<token>.json (using SQLite history + intraday tick bars) so
    StockMicroBot ML inference is active from 09:15:00 AM.
    """
    import sqlite3
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import lightgbm as lgb
    from src.ml.bot_trainer import build_sqlite_fallback_profile, FEATURE_COLS

    os.makedirs(BOTS_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    db_path = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "master_history.db")

    sym_map = {}
    if os.path.exists(db_path):
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
            for tok_r, sym_r in conn.execute("SELECT DISTINCT token, symbol FROM historical_data").fetchall():
                sym_map[str(tok_r)] = str(sym_r)
            conn.close()
        except Exception:
            pass

    target_map = {}
    if os.path.exists(TARGET_TICKS_FILE):
        try:
            with open(TARGET_TICKS_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                for item in json.load(f):
                    target_map[str(item.get("token"))] = item
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Build 2-minute bars per stock from the tick stream for instant warmup & fallback LightGBM training
    tick_bars_by_sec = {}
    stock_prices = {}
    if ticks:
        for t in ticks:
            sid = str(t["security_id"])
            if sid in EXCLUDED_TOKENS:
                continue
            ltp = float(t["ltp"])
            ltq = int(t["ltq"])
            if ltp <= 0:
                continue
            stock_prices[sid] = ltp
            bucket = (int(t["timestamp"]) // 1000) // 60 * 60  # 1-min buckets for training richness
            bars_dict = tick_bars_by_sec.setdefault(sid, {})
            b = bars_dict.get(bucket)
            if b is None:
                bars_dict[bucket] = {"open": ltp, "high": ltp, "low": ltp, "close": ltp, "vol": max(1, ltq)}
            else:
                if ltp > b["high"]: b["high"] = ltp
                if ltp < b["low"]: b["low"] = ltp
                b["close"] = ltp
                b["vol"] += max(0, ltq)

    calibrated = 0
    ml_ready = 0
    for sec_id in sorted(security_ids):
        tok = str(sec_id)
        if tok in EXCLUDED_TOKENS:
            continue

        prof_path = os.path.join(BOTS_DIR, f"profile_{tok}.json")
        model_path = os.path.join(BOTS_DIR, f"bot_{tok}.txt")

        sym_name = target_map.get(tok, {}).get("symbol") or sym_map.get(tok) or f"TOK_{tok}"
        item = target_map.get(tok, {"token": tok, "symbol": sym_name, "master_score": 0.65, "ai_score": 0.55})
        item["symbol"] = sym_name
        prof = build_sqlite_fallback_profile(tok, item)

        p_now = float(stock_prices.get(tok, prof.get("median_price", 500.0)))
        prof["median_price"] = round(p_now, 2)
        prof["cvd_threshold"] = round(max(50.0, 100000.0 / max(p_now, 1.0)), 1)
        prof["obi_threshold"] = 0.20
        prof["sl_pct"] = 0.012
        prof["tp_pct"] = 0.024
        prof["breakeven_pct"] = 0.0075
        prof["partial_tp_pct"] = 0.012
        prof["vwap_buffer_pct"] = 0.0020

        # Extract warmup bars and train LightGBM model if needed
        b_dict = tick_bars_by_sec.get(tok, {})
        sorted_buckets = [b_dict[k] for k in sorted(b_dict.keys())]
        if len(sorted_buckets) >= 15:
            prof["warmup_bars"] = sorted_buckets[:15]

        if not (os.path.exists(model_path) and os.path.getsize(model_path) > 500):
            try:
                df_src = pd.DataFrame(sorted_buckets) if len(sorted_buckets) >= 50 else pd.DataFrame()
                if df_src.empty and os.path.exists(db_path):
                    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
                    df_src = pd.read_sql_query(
                        "SELECT open, high, low, close, volume as vol FROM historical_data WHERE token = ? ORDER BY timestamp ASC LIMIT 250",
                        conn, params=(tok,)
                    )
                    conn.close()

                if len(df_src) >= 45:
                    df_src["ret_2m"] = df_src["close"].pct_change(1)
                    df_src["ret_6m"] = df_src["close"].pct_change(3)
                    df_src["ret_16m"] = df_src["close"].pct_change(8)
                    tr = (df_src["high"] - df_src["low"]).replace(0, 1e-4)
                    df_src["atr_14"] = tr.rolling(10).mean().bfill()
                    df_src["atr_pct"] = (df_src["atr_14"] / df_src["close"]).clip(0.0005, 0.05)
                    df_src["vwap_dist_atr"] = ((df_src["close"] - df_src["close"].rolling(10).mean().bfill()) / df_src["atr_14"]).clip(-5.0, 5.0)
                    df_src["rvol_2m"] = (df_src["vol"] / df_src["vol"].rolling(15).mean().bfill().replace(0, 1.0)).clip(0.0, 10.0)
                    df_src["body_ratio"] = ((df_src["close"] - df_src["open"]) / tr).clip(-1.0, 1.0)
                    delta = df_src["close"].diff()
                    gain = delta.clip(lower=0.0).rolling(10).mean().bfill()
                    loss = (-delta.clip(upper=0.0)).rolling(10).mean().bfill().replace(0, 1e-6)
                    df_src["rsi_14"] = (1.0 - (1.0 / (1.0 + (gain / loss)))).clip(0.0, 1.0)
                    df_src.dropna(subset=FEATURE_COLS, inplace=True)

                    fwd = df_src["close"].pct_change(4).shift(-4).iloc[:-4]
                    X_df = df_src[FEATURE_COLS].iloc[:-4]
                    q_low, q_high = float(fwd.quantile(0.30)), float(fwd.quantile(0.70))
                    y = np.select([fwd <= q_low, fwd >= q_high], [0, 2], default=1).astype(int)
                    if len(np.unique(y)) == 3 and len(X_df) >= 30:
                        ds = lgb.Dataset(X_df.values.astype(np.float32), label=y, feature_name=FEATURE_COLS)
                        bst = lgb.train(
                            {"objective": "multiclass", "num_class": 3, "max_depth": 3, "num_leaves": 7, "min_data_in_leaf": 5, "learning_rate": 0.05, "verbose": -1, "seed": 42},
                            ds, num_boost_round=35
                        )
                        bst.save_model(model_path)
            except Exception:
                pass

        prof["has_ml_model"] = os.path.exists(model_path) and os.path.getsize(model_path) > 500
        if prof["has_ml_model"]:
            ml_ready += 1

        with open(prof_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as pf:
            json.dump(prof, pf, indent=4)
        calibrated += 1

    print(f"[BOT CHECK] Calibrated {calibrated} StockMicroBots ({ml_ready} LightGBM ML Models + Warmup Bars Ready).")


def load_stream(ticks: list[dict], r: redis.Redis):
    total = len(ticks)
    t0 = time.time()
    r.delete(REPLAY_STREAM)
    r.set("replay_mode", "stream")

    pipe = r.pipeline(transaction=False)
    for i, tick in enumerate(ticks):
        pipe.xadd(REPLAY_STREAM, {"data": json.dumps(tick)})
        if (i + 1) % 1000 == 0:
            pipe.execute()
            pipe = r.pipeline(transaction=False)
    pipe.execute()
    elapsed = max(0.001, time.time() - t0)
    print(f"[STREAM READY] Loaded {total:,} ticks into Redis Stream in {elapsed:.1f}s ({total/elapsed:,.0f} ticks/sec)")


def print_post_replay_summary():
    today_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    csv_path = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "src", "trading", f"paper_trades_{today_str}.csv")
    state = {"current_balance": 100000.0, "initial_balance": 100000.0, "peak_capital_deployed": 0.0, "total_turnover_deployed": 0.0}
    if os.path.exists(PORTFOLIO_STATE_FILE):
        with open(PORTFOLIO_STATE_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            state.update(json.load(f))

    bal = float(state.get("current_balance", 100000.0))
    init_bal = float(state.get("initial_balance", 100000.0))
    peak_cap = float(state.get("peak_capital_deployed", 0.0))
    turnover = float(state.get("total_turnover_deployed", 0.0))
    net_pnl = bal - init_bal
    roi_active = (net_pnl / peak_cap * 100.0) if peak_cap > 0 else 0.0

    print("\n" + "=" * 65)
    print("  FULL-PIPELINE REPLAY & CAPITAL UTILIZATION SUMMARY")
    print("=" * 65)
    print(f"  Final Portfolio Balance : Rs.{bal:,.2f} (Net PnL: Rs.{net_pnl:+,.2f} | {net_pnl/init_bal*100:+.2f}%)")
    print(f"  Peak Active Capital     : Rs.{peak_cap:,.2f} (ROI on Active Cap: {roi_active:+.2f}%)")
    print(f"  Total Turnover Deployed : Rs.{turnover:,.2f}")
    if os.path.exists(csv_path):
        with open(csv_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
        closed = [r for r in rows if float(r.get("gross_pnl", 0)) != 0]
        wins = [r for r in closed if float(r.get("net_pnl", 0)) > 0]
        gross_sum = sum(float(r.get("gross_pnl", 0)) for r in rows)
        tax_sum = sum(float(r.get("transaction_taxes", 0)) for r in rows)
        print(f"  Gross PnL vs Taxes      : Gross Rs.{gross_sum:+,.2f} | Taxes -Rs.{tax_sum:,.2f}")
        print(f"  Total Executions (Legs) : {len(rows)}")
        print(f"  Closed Round-Trips      : {len(closed)} (Wins: {len(wins)} | Win Rate: {(len(wins)/len(closed)*100) if closed else 0:.1f}%)")
        print(f"  CSV Ledger              : {csv_path}")
    print("=" * 65 + "\n")


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Full-Pipeline Replay System")
    parser.add_argument("--date", type=str, nargs="?", const=None, default=None, help="Date in YYYYMMDD format")
    parser.add_argument("--file", type=str, default=None, help="Explicit path to .sql.gz backup")
    parser.add_argument("--fast", action="store_true", help="Load Redis stream for fast replay")
    parser.add_argument("--full", action="store_true", help="Run full pipeline (Ensure Bots -> Load Stream -> Run Strategy Engine)")
    parser.add_argument("--train-bots", action="store_true", help="Force live 60-day Angel API bot training before replay")
    parser.add_argument("--reset", action="store_true", help="Reset portfolio to Rs.1,00,000 and clear today's trade CSV")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    date_str = args.date or datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    filepath = find_backup_file(date_str, args.file)

    r = redis.Redis(host=os.getenv("REDIS_HOST", "localhost"), port=int(os.getenv("REDIS_PORT", 6379)), decode_responses=True)
    r.ping()

    ticks, detected_date = parse_sql_gz(filepath, args.date)
    securities = set(t["security_id"] for t in ticks)

    if args.reset:
        with open(PORTFOLIO_STATE_FILE, "w") as f:
            json.dump({"current_balance": 100000.0}, f)
        today_csv = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "src", "trading", f"paper_trades_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}.csv")
        if os.path.exists(today_csv):
            os.remove(today_csv)
        print("[RESET] Portfolio reset to Rs.1,00,000.00 & cleared today's paper_trades CSV.")

    if args.train_bots:
        subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "src.ml.bot_trainer"], check=False)

    ensure_bots_for_replay(securities, ticks)

    if args.full or args.fast:
        load_stream(ticks, r)
        if args.full:
            print("\n[AUTO-RUN] Launching Strategy Engine on Replay Stream...\n")
            subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "src.trading.strategy_engine"], check=False)
            print_post_replay_summary()
        else:
            print("\n>> Now run: python -m src.trading.strategy_engine\n")
    else:
        replay_paced(ticks, r)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

pkill -f "python3 -m src.trading.strategy_engine" || true

tmux kill-session -t hft_live 2>/dev/null || true

tmux split-window -t hft_live -v
tmux send-keys -t hft_live "cd $PROJECT_DIR && source venv/bin/activate && python3 -m src.trading.strategy_engine" C-m

============================================================

import json
import os

PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
TARGET_FILE = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "target_ticks.json")
SYMBOLS_FILE = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "symbols.json")

SCREENER_LIMIT = 25
FALLBACK_TOKENS = ["2885", "1333", "3045"]  # Reliance, HDFC Bank, SBI safety fallback

def update():
    token_list = []

    if not os.path.exists(TARGET_FILE):
        print("[WARN] target_ticks.json not found. Using fallback equity tokens.")
        token_list = list(FALLBACK_TOKENS)
    else:
        with open(TARGET_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            target_data = json.load(f)

        for item in target_data:
            if len(token_list) >= SCREENER_LIMIT:
                break
            tok = str(item.get("token", "")).strip()
            if tok and tok not in token_list:
                token_list.append(tok)

    output_data = {"tokens": token_list}
    with open(SYMBOLS_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(output_data, f, indent=4)

    print(f"[SYMBOLS LOCKED] Loaded {len(token_list)} active equity tokens into symbols.json")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    update()
