"""
overnight_rumor_worker.py
Midnight Shift Harvester (High-Capacity Edition)
Scrapes 50-100+ market articles across major Indian financial feeds,
normalizes exact NSE symbols, scores catalyst intensity via Gemini/Groq,
and exports sorted rumors to data/overnight_rumors.json.
"""

import os
import json
import time
import logging
import feedparser
from typing import List, Dict, Optional
from dotenv import load_dotenv

logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-7s | %(name)-15s | %(message)s"
)
logger = logging.getLogger("MidnightHarvester")

load_dotenv()
GEMINI_API_KEY = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY")
GROQ_API_KEY = os.getenv("GROQ_API_KEY")

OUTPUT_PATH = "data/overnight_rumors.json"

class OvernightHarvester:
    def __init__(self):
        # Multi-source RSS network for broad Indian market coverage
        self.rss_urls = [
            "https://economictimes.indiatimes.com/markets/rssfeeds/2146842.cms",          # ET Markets
            "https://economictimes.indiatimes.com/markets/stocks/rssfeeds/2146843.cms",   # ET Stocks
            "https://www.moneycontrol.com/rss/marketreports.xml",                         # Moneycontrol Markets
            "https://www.moneycontrol.com/rss/business.xml",                              # Moneycontrol Business
            "https://www.livemint.com/rss/markets",                                       # LiveMint Markets
            "https://www.business-standard.com/rss/markets-106.rss",                      # Business Standard
            "https://www.financialexpress.com/market/feed/"                               # Financial Express
        ]
        self.rumors = []

    def scrape_all_feeds(self, target_min: int = 50, target_max: int = 120):
        logger.info(f"Initiating high-capacity RSS scrape across {len(self.rss_urls)} channels...")
        seen_titles = set()

        for url in self.rss_urls:
            try:
                feed = feedparser.parse(url)
                # Pull up to 25 items per feed
                for entry in feed.entries[:25]:
                    title_clean = entry.title.strip()
                    title_key = title_clean.lower()[:60]  # Deduplicate cross-published headlines
                    
                    if title_key not in seen_titles:
                        seen_titles.add(title_key)
                        self.rumors.append({
                            "source": "RSS",
                            "title": title_clean,
                            "summary": entry.get("summary", "")[:600],
                            "url": entry.get("link", "")
                        })
                    if len(self.rumors) >= target_max:
                        break
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"Failed to fetch feed {url}: {e}")

            if len(self.rumors) >= target_max:
                break

        logger.info(f"Harvested {len(self.rumors)} unique financial articles (Target: {target_min}-{target_max}).")

class RumorEvaluator:
    def __init__(self):
        self.gemini_url = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.5-flash:generateContent"
        self.groq_url = "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions"
        self.gemini_exhausted = False

    def _call_groq(self, prompt: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        if not GROQ_API_KEY:
            logger.error("GROQ_API_KEY missing.")
            return None
        try:
            import httpx
            headers = {
                "Authorization": f"Bearer {GROQ_API_KEY}",
                "Content-Type": "application/json"
            }
            payload = {
                "model": "llama-3.1-8b-instant",
                "messages": [
                    {
                        "role": "system", 
                        "content": "You are a financial catalyst engine for the National Stock Exchange of India (NSE). Always output valid JSON only with keys: symbol, sentiment_bias, rumor_intensity, reasoning."
                    },
                    {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
                ],
                "temperature": 0.1,
                "response_format": {"type": "json_object"}
            }
            res = httpx.post(self.groq_url, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=12.0)
            if res.status_code == 200:
                raw_json = res.json()["choices"][0]["message"]["content"].strip()
                return json.loads(raw_json)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"Groq call exception: {e}")
        return None

    def _call_gemini(self, prompt: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        try:
            import httpx
            headers = {
                "x-goog-api-key": GEMINI_API_KEY,
                "Content-Type": "application/json"
            }
            payload = {"contents": [{"parts": [{"text": prompt}]}]}
            res = httpx.post(self.gemini_url, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=12.0)
            if res.status_code == 200:
                raw = res.json()["candidates"][0]["content"]["parts"][0]["text"].strip()
                if raw.startswith("```"):
                    raw = raw.strip("`").replace("json\n", "", 1).strip()
                return json.loads(raw)
            elif res.status_code in (429, 503, 500, 502):
                logger.warning(f"[CIRCUIT BREAKER] Gemini {res.status_code} encountered. Switching to Groq for remainder.")
                self.gemini_exhausted = True
                return None
            else:
                return None
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"[CIRCUIT BREAKER] Gemini connection error ({e}). Switching to Groq.")
            self.gemini_exhausted = True
            return None

    def evaluate_and_stream(self, raw_data: List[Dict]):
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(OUTPUT_PATH), exist_ok=True)
        results = []
        if os.path.exists(OUTPUT_PATH):
            try:
                with open(OUTPUT_PATH, "r") as f:
                    results = json.load(f)
            except Exception:
                results = []

        seen_symbols = {r.get("symbol") for r in results if r.get("symbol")}

        total_items = len(raw_data)
        logger.info(f"Evaluating {total_items} items with live incremental ranking...")

        for idx, item in enumerate(raw_data, 1):
            prompt = f"""Analyze this Indian financial headline and extract company-specific catalysts:
Title: {item['title']}
Body: {item['summary']}

Requirements:
1. "symbol": Exact NSE trading ticker symbol (e.g. RELIANCE, TATAMOTORS, ALOKINDS, HDFCBANK). If broad indices (NIFTY/SENSEX), US stocks, or no specific listed Indian stock, output "NONE".
2. "sentiment_bias": "BULLISH", "BEARISH", or "NEUTRAL".
3. "rumor_intensity": Float from 0.0 to 1.0 (0.9 = block deal / leak / massive contract, 0.5 = quarterly earnings, 0.1 = broad commentary).
4. "reasoning": 1 concise sentence explaining the catalyst.

JSON format only:"""

            parsed = None
            if not self.gemini_exhausted:
                parsed = self._call_gemini(prompt)

            if not parsed:
                parsed = self._call_groq(prompt)

            # Normalize single dict, list of dicts, or nested wrapper dict into a flat list
            candidates = []
            if isinstance(parsed, list):
                candidates = [x for x in parsed if isinstance(x, dict)]
            elif isinstance(parsed, dict):
                if "symbol" in parsed:
                    candidates = [parsed]
                else:
                    for val in parsed.values():
                        if isinstance(val, list):
                            candidates.extend([x for x in val if isinstance(x, dict)])

            extracted_any = False
            for entry in candidates:
                try:
                    raw_sym = entry.get("symbol")
                    if not raw_sym or not isinstance(raw_sym, str):
                        continue
                    sym = raw_sym.upper().strip().replace(" ", "")
                    if sym in ("NONE", "", "NULL", "N/A", "NIFTY", "SENSEX"):
                        continue
                    if sym == "ALOK":
                        sym = "ALOKINDS"

                    extracted_any = True
                    if sym not in seen_symbols:
                        seen_symbols.add(sym)
                        entry["symbol"] = sym
                        entry["sentiment_bias"] = str(entry.get("sentiment_bias", "NEUTRAL")).upper().strip()
                        entry["rumor_intensity"] = float(entry.get("rumor_intensity", 0.5))
                        entry["reasoning"] = str(entry.get("reasoning", "Overnight news catalyst.")).strip()
                        results.append(entry)

                        results.sort(key=lambda x: x.get("rumor_intensity", 0.0), reverse=True)
                        with open(OUTPUT_PATH, "w") as f:
                            json.dump(results, f, indent=4)
                        logger.info(f"[{idx}/{total_items}] Added {sym} (Intensity: {entry['rumor_intensity']}) -> {OUTPUT_PATH}")
                    else:
                        logger.info(f"[{idx}/{total_items}] Duplicate ticker {sym} skipped.")
                except Exception as item_err:
                    logger.warning(f"[{idx}/{total_items}] Skipped malformed item: {item_err}")

            if not extracted_any:
                logger.info(f"[{idx}/{total_items}] No company-specific ticker identified.")

            time.sleep(1.5)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    harvester = OvernightHarvester()
    harvester.scrape_all_feeds(target_min=50, target_max=100)

    evaluator = RumorEvaluator()
    evaluator.evaluate_and_stream(harvester.rumors)
    logger.info(f"Overnight Shift Complete. Processed {len(harvester.rumors)} headlines.")

FILE: .\src\scripts\screener.py
============================================================
"""
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  PRE-MARKET ENSEMBLE SCREENER
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Daily pipeline:
  1. Authenticate with Angel One API
  2. Download/sync OHLCV history for all NSE equities → SQLite
  3. Pre-filter: remove penny stocks, illiquid stocks
  4. Score each surviving stock with 19-scorer weighted ensemble
  5. Rank → write CSV (full universe) + JSON (top N for trading)
  6. Dispatch to Telegram

Output flow:
  screener.py → target_ticks.json → update_symbols.py → symbols.json → WebSocket/Redis
"""

import os
import sys
import json
import time
import sqlite3
import gc
import pyotp
import requests
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from SmartApi import SmartConnect
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

    sys.path.insert(0, project_root)

class MasterScreener:
    def __init__(self, db_path: str):
        self.db_path = db_path

        self.scorers = {
            "volatility": [
                HistoricalVolatilityScorer(),
                GapRiskScorer(),
                NormalizedATRScorer(),
                BollingerBandWidthScorer(),
                IntradayAmplitudeScorer()
            ],
            "liquidity": [
                ChaikinMoneyFlowScorer(),
                VolumePriceTrendScorer(),
                RelativeVolumeScorer(),
                AverageDailyTurnoverScorer(),
            ],
            "momentum": [
                MovingAverageAlignmentScorer(),
                RateOfChangeScorer(),
                DistanceFromHighScorer(),
                RSIScorer(),
                AverageDirectionalIndexScorer()
            ],
            "exhaustion": [
                BollingerPctBScorer(),
                PriceToEMAStretchScorer()
            ],
            "microstructure": [
                DecisiveBodyScorer(),
                ConsecutiveDaysStreakScorer(),
                ClosingRangeScorer()
            ]
        }

        self.category_weights = {
            "volatility": 0.20,
            "liquidity": 0.25,
            "momentum": 0.35,
            "exhaustion": 0.10,
            "microstructure": 0.10
        }

    def fetch_data(self, token: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """
        Fetches the LATEST 252 trading days for a stock.

        CRITICAL FIX: Previous version used ORDER BY timestamp ASC LIMIT 252,
        which returned the OLDEST 252 days. New data was never seen, which is
        why the screener gave the same list every day.

        Now uses ORDER BY DESC to get the most recent data, then reverses
        to chronological order for the scoring modules.
        """
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        query = """
            SELECT timestamp, open, high, low, close, volume 
            FROM historical_data 
            WHERE token = ? 
            ORDER BY timestamp DESC 
            LIMIT 252
        """
        df = pd.read_sql_query(query, conn, params=(token,))
        conn.close()

        if df.empty:
            return df

        # Reverse from newest-first → chronological (oldest-first) as scorers expect
        df = df.iloc[::-1].reset_index(drop=True)

        df['timestamp'] = pd.to_datetime(df['timestamp'])
        for col in ['open', 'high', 'low', 'close', 'volume']:
            df[col] = df[col].astype(float)

        return df

    def pre_filter(self, token: str) -> dict:
        """
        Quick pre-filter check BEFORE running the expensive 19-scorer ensemble.
        Returns a dict with filter results, or None if the stock should be skipped.

        Filters:
          - Minimum 100 days of data (relaxed from 252 to include more stocks)
          - Price >= Rs.10 (no penny stocks — untradeable spreads)
          - Average daily turnover >= Rs.50 lakh (minimum liquidity for intraday)
        """
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()

        # Get the most recent 20 days for quick checks
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT close, volume FROM historical_data 
            WHERE token = ? ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 20
        """, (token,))
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        conn.close()

        if len(rows) < 20:
            return None

        latest_price = float(rows[0][0])
        avg_volume = sum(float(r[1]) for r in rows) / len(rows)
        avg_turnover = latest_price * avg_volume

        # Hard filters for intraday tradability
        if latest_price < 10.0:
            return None   # Penny stocks have spreads wider than their price movement
        if avg_turnover < 5_000_000:
            return None   # Less than Rs.50 lakh/day = can't enter/exit cleanly

        return {
            "price": latest_price,
            "avg_volume": avg_volume,
            "avg_turnover": avg_turnover
        }

    def evaluate_stock(self, token: str, symbol: str) -> dict:
        """Runs the full 19-scorer ensemble on a single stock."""
        df = self.fetch_data(token)

        if len(df) < 100:
            return {"symbol": symbol, "token": token, "master_score": 0.0, "status": "Insufficient Data"}

        output = {
            "symbol": symbol,
            "token": token
        }

        category_scores = {}

        for category, scorer_list in self.scorers.items():
            scores = []
            for scorer in scorer_list:
                try:
                    val = scorer.calculate(df)
                    scores.append(val)
                    output[scorer.__class__.__name__] = round(val, 4)
                except Exception as e:
                    # Don't let one broken scorer kill the entire stock
                    scores.append(0.5)  # Neutral fallback
                    output[scorer.__class__.__name__] = 0.5

            cat_avg = sum(scores) / len(scores) if scores else 0.0
            category_scores[category] = cat_avg
            output[f"CAT_{category.upper()}"] = round(cat_avg, 4)

        # Apply Ensemble Weighting
        master_score = sum(category_scores[cat] * weight for cat, weight in self.category_weights.items())
        output["master_score"] = round(master_score, 4)

        return output

    def run_screener(self, stock_list: List[Dict[str, str]], batch_size: int = 500) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Scores all stocks and returns a ranked DataFrame."""
        results = []
        total_stocks = len(stock_list)
        filtered_out = 0
        evaluated = 0

        print(f"[PHASE 2] Initializing Ensemble Scan for {total_stocks} equities...")

        for i in range(0, total_stocks, batch_size):
            batch = stock_list[i:i + batch_size]
            print(f"--> Processing batch {i // batch_size + 1} ({len(batch)} stocks)...")

            for stock in batch:
                try:
                    # Pre-filter: skip penny stocks and illiquid names
                    filter_result = self.pre_filter(stock['token'])
                    if filter_result is None:
                        filtered_out += 1
                        continue

                    score_data = self.evaluate_stock(stock['token'], stock['symbol'])
                    if score_data.get('master_score', 0) > 0:
                        # Attach price/turnover for reference
                        score_data['latest_price'] = round(filter_result['price'], 2)
                        score_data['avg_daily_turnover'] = round(filter_result['avg_turnover'], 0)
                        results.append(score_data)
                        evaluated += 1
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"Mathematical Fault on {stock['symbol']}: {e}")
                    continue

            gc.collect()
            time.sleep(0.5)

        print(f"[PHASE 2] Pre-filter removed {filtered_out} stocks (penny/illiquid)")
        print(f"[PHASE 2] Evaluated {evaluated} stocks with full ensemble")

        results_df = pd.DataFrame(results)
        if not results_df.empty:
            for cat, weight in self.category_weights.items():
                col = f"CAT_{cat.upper()}"
                if col in results_df.columns:
                    results_df[f"{col}_PCT"] = results_df[col].rank(pct=True)
            pct_cols = [f"CAT_{c.upper()}_PCT" for c in self.category_weights if f"CAT_{c.upper()}_PCT" in results_df.columns]
            if len(pct_cols) == len(self.category_weights):
                results_df["raw_master_score"] = results_df["master_score"]
                results_df["master_score"] = sum(
                    results_df[f"CAT_{cat.upper()}_PCT"] * w for cat, w in self.category_weights.items()
                ).round(4)
            results_df = results_df.sort_values(by="master_score", ascending=False).reset_index(drop=True)

        return results_df


class PreMarketOrchestrator:
    def __init__(self, top_n=50):
        self.top_n = top_n
        self.db_path = os.path.join(project_root, 'master_history.db')
        self.watchlist_path = os.path.join(project_root, 'target_ticks.json')
        self.csv_path = os.path.join(project_root, 'live_screener_rankings.csv')
        self.surviving_equities = []

        self.api = self._authenticate()

    def _authenticate(self):
        """Silently authenticates with Angel One using PyOTP."""
        api_key = os.getenv("ANGEL_API_KEY")
        client_id = os.getenv("ANGEL_CLIENT_ID")
        pin = os.getenv("ANGEL_PIN")
        totp_secret = os.getenv("ANGEL_TOTP_SECRET")

        if not all([api_key, client_id, pin, totp_secret]):
            raise ValueError("[FATAL] Missing Angel One credentials in .env file.")

        try:
            print(f"[SYSTEM] Authenticating Angel One API for Client: {client_id}...")
            api = SmartConnect(api_key=api_key)
            totp = pyotp.TOTP(totp_secret).now()
            session = api.generateSession(client_id, pin, totp)

            if session.get('status') is False:
                raise PermissionError(f"Authentication Rejected: {session.get('message')}")

            print("[SUCCESS] Angel One Session Established.")
            return api

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[ERROR] API Authentication Failed: {e}")
            exit(1)

    def fetch_master_contract_list(self):
        """Downloads and filters the daily instrument master list."""
        print("[SYSTEM] Fetching master contract list from Angel One...")
        url = "https://margincalculator.angelbroking.com/OpenAPI_File/files/OpenAPIScripMaster.json"

        try:
            response = requests.get(url, timeout=10)
            response.raise_for_status()
            data = response.json()

            self.surviving_equities = [
                item for item in data
                if item.get('exch_seg') == 'NSE'
                and item.get('symbol', '').endswith('-EQ')
            ]

            print(f"[SUCCESS] Downloaded {len(data)} total instruments.")
            print(f"[SYSTEM] Liquidity Purge Complete. Surviving NSE Equities: {len(self.surviving_equities)}")

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[ERROR] Failed to fetch or parse master contract list: {e}")
            exit(1)

    def validate_database(self):
        """Checks the database has recent data before scoring."""
        if not os.path.exists(self.db_path):
            print("[FATAL] master_history.db not found. Run ingester first.")
            return False

        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()

        try:
            cursor.execute("SELECT MAX(timestamp), COUNT(*), COUNT(DISTINCT token) FROM historical_data")
            latest, total_rows, total_tokens = cursor.fetchone()

            if total_rows == 0:
                print("[FATAL] Database is empty. Ingester has not run yet.")
                return False

            print(f"[DB CHECK] {total_rows:,} rows | {total_tokens} tokens | Latest: {latest}")

            # Warn if data is stale (> 3 days old)
            if latest:
                latest_dt = datetime.strptime(str(latest)[:10], '%Y-%m-%d')
                days_old = (datetime.now() - latest_dt).days
                if days_old > 3:
                    print(f"[WARNING] Database is {days_old} days stale! Latest data: {latest}")
                    print(f"[WARNING] Scores may not reflect current market conditions.")
                else:
                    print(f"[DB CHECK] Data is {days_old} day(s) old. OK.")

            return True

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[ERROR] Database validation failed: {e}")
            return False
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def execute_daily_pipeline(self):
        # 1. Fetch live universe
        self.fetch_master_contract_list()

        # 2. Sync SQLite Database
        print("\n[PHASE 1] Initializing Master Database Ingestion...")
        ingester = MasterIngester(api_client=self.api, db_path=self.db_path)
        ingester.sync_database(self.surviving_equities)

        # 2b. Validate database has usable data
        if not self.validate_database():
            print("[FATAL] Cannot proceed with empty or corrupt database.")
            return

        # 3. Run Ensemble Scoring
        screener = MasterScreener(db_path=self.db_path)
        results_df = screener.run_screener(self.surviving_equities, batch_size=500)

        if results_df.empty:
            print("[FATAL] Screener returned no valid results. Check DB and guardrails.")
            print("[FATAL] NOT overwriting target_ticks.json to preserve yesterday's picks.")
            return

        # 4. Generate CSV
        print(f"\n[PHASE 3] Generating Output Artifacts...")
        results_df.to_csv(self.csv_path, index=False)
        print(f"--> [SUCCESS] Full universe rankings saved: {self.csv_path}")
        print(f"--> Top 5 by master_score:")
        for _, row in results_df.head(5).iterrows():
            print(f"    {row['symbol']:>20} | score: {row['master_score']:.4f} | price: Rs.{row.get('latest_price', '?')}")

        # 5. Generate JSON Handoff (Overwrites daily for execution engine)
        top_stocks = results_df.head(self.top_n)
        final_watchlist = []
        for _, row in top_stocks.iterrows():
            final_watchlist.append({
                "symbol": row['symbol'],
                "token": row['token'],
                "master_score": row['master_score']
            })

        with open(self.watchlist_path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(final_watchlist, f, indent=4)
        print(f"--> [SUCCESS] Top {self.top_n} targets written: {self.watchlist_path}")

        # 6. Telegram Handoff
        try:
            print("--> [SYSTEM] Executing Telegram pre-market dispatch...")
            reporter = TelegramReporter()
            reporter.send_file(self.csv_path)
            reporter.send_premarket_watchlist(self.watchlist_path)
            print("--> [SUCCESS] Telegram dispatch complete.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"--> [ERROR] Telegram handoff crashed: {e}")

        print(f"\n{'='*55}")
        print(f"  SCREENER COMPLETE — {len(final_watchlist)} stocks selected")
        print(f"  Next step: update_symbols.py picks top 12 → symbols.json")
        print(f"{'='*55}\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("\n================================================")
    print("   BOOTING PRE-MARKET ENSEMBLE ORCHESTRATOR")
    print("================================================\n")

    orchestrator = PreMarketOrchestrator(top_n=50)
    orchestrator.execute_daily_pipeline()

import os
import sys
import glob
import json
import shutil
import asyncio
import subprocess
import aiohttp
import redis.asyncio as aioredis
from datetime import datetime
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from telegram import Update
from telegram.ext import ApplicationBuilder, CommandHandler, ContextTypes

current_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
project_root = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(current_dir, "../../"))
if project_root not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, project_root)

load_dotenv(os.path.join(project_root, ".env"))

from src.utils.api_tracker import APITracker
from src.scripts.telegram_reporter import TelegramReporter

TOKEN = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN")
DEFAULT_CHAT_ID = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID")

TIERS = {
    1: os.getenv("TIER1_CHAT_ID") or DEFAULT_CHAT_ID,
    2: os.getenv("TIER2_CHAT_ID") or DEFAULT_CHAT_ID,
    3: os.getenv("TIER3_CHAT_ID") or DEFAULT_CHAT_ID,
}

base_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(current_dir), "trading")
portfolio_state_path = os.path.join(base_dir, "portfolio_state.json")
bots_dir = os.path.join(project_root, "models", "bots")


def get_redis_client():
    return aioredis.Redis(
        host=os.getenv("REDIS_HOST", "localhost"),
        port=int(os.getenv("REDIS_PORT", 6379)),
        decode_responses=True,
        socket_connect_timeout=5,
        health_check_interval=30,
    )


def get_disk_state() -> dict:
    if os.path.exists(portfolio_state_path):
        try:
            with open(portfolio_state_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                return json.load(f)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return {"current_balance": 100000.0, "initial_balance": 100000.0, "peak_capital_deployed": 0.0}


async def start_command(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    await update.message.reply_text(
        "🤖 <b>Quantitative Trading C2 Node (Azure)</b>\n"
        "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
        "📊 /dashboard - Active vs Idle Capital, Margin & Live Positions\n"
        "💓 /status - System Heartbeat, RAM/Disk & Process Health\n"
        "🧠 /bots - Active 25 Per-Stock Micro-Bot Profiles\n"
        "🔢 /quota - Angel One / Dhan / LLM API Quota Counter\n"
        "📈 /eod - Full EOD Capital Utilization & Attribution Report\n"
        "📄 /logs - Download today's signal log\n"
        "📑 /trades - Download today's CSV trade ledger\n"
        "🔍 /screener - Trigger EOD Quant Screener in background\n"
        "🔄 /restart - Restart live 3-pane Tmux Engine\n"
        "🚨 /kill - EMERGENCY STOP (Flattens all positions)",
        parse_mode="HTML"
    )


async def dashboard_command(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    r_cmd = get_redis_client()
    try:
        disk_state = get_disk_state()
        init_bal = float(disk_state.get("initial_balance", 100000.0))
        max_slots = int(os.getenv("MAX_CONCURRENT_POSITIONS", 4))

        balance = await r_cmd.get("live_balance")
        pos_data = await r_cmd.get("live_positions")
        last_tick = await r_cmd.get("last_tick_ist")
        hb = await r_cmd.get("engine_heartbeat")

        current_balance = float(balance) if balance else float(disk_state.get("current_balance", 100000.0))
        positions = json.loads(pos_data) if (pos_data and pos_data != "{}") else {}

        active_notional = sum(float(p["qty"]) * float(p["avg_price"]) for p in positions.values())
        margin_locked = active_notional / 5.0
        idle_capital = max(0.0, current_balance - margin_locked)
        peak_active = max(active_notional, float(disk_state.get("peak_capital_deployed", 0.0)))

        net_pnl = current_balance - init_bal
        roi_total = (net_pnl / init_bal * 100.0) if init_bal > 0 else 0.0
        roi_active = (net_pnl / peak_active * 100.0) if peak_active > 0 else 0.0
        icon = "🟢" if net_pnl >= 0 else "🔴"
        engine_status = "🟢 LIVE" if hb else "🌙 STANDBY"

        msg = (
            f"🏦 <b>Portfolio & Capital Dashboard ({engine_status})</b>\n"
            f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
            f"💰 <b>Total Equity:</b> ₹{current_balance:,.2f}\n"
            f"{icon} <b>Realized Net PnL:</b> ₹{net_pnl:+,.2f} ({roi_total:+.2f}%)\n"
            f"🎯 <b>ROI on Active Capital:</b> {roi_active:+.2f}%\n"
            f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
            f"⚡ <b>Active Deployed (Notional):</b> ₹{active_notional:,.2f}\n"
            f"🔒 <b>Margin Used (5x Intraday):</b> ₹{margin_locked:,.2f}\n"
            f"🟢 <b>Remaining / Idle Capital:</b> ₹{idle_capital:,.2f}\n"
            f"🔥 <b>Peak Active Capital Today:</b> ₹{peak_active:,.2f}\n"
            f"🎰 <b>Active Slots:</b> <code>{len(positions)} / {max_slots}</code>\n"
        )
        if last_tick:
            msg += f"⏱ <b>Last Tick:</b> <code>{last_tick} IST</code>\n"
        msg += "\n"

        if not positions:
            msg += "📭 <i>Portfolio is flat (100% capital idle).</i>"
        else:
            msg += "📊 <b>Open Positions:</b>\n"
            for sec_id, data in positions.items():
                sym = data.get("symbol", f"TOK_{sec_id}")
                notional = float(data["qty"]) * float(data["avg_price"])
                src = data.get("trigger_source", "BOT")
                msg += (
                    f"• <code>{sym}</code> (#{sec_id}) | <b>{data['side']}</b> {data['qty']}x "
                    f"@ ₹{data['avg_price']:.2f} (₹{notional:,.0f}) [{src}]\n"
                )

        await update.message.reply_text(msg, parse_mode="HTML")
    finally:
        await r_cmd.aclose()


async def status_command(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    r_cmd = get_redis_client()
    try:
        hb_raw = await r_cmd.get("engine_heartbeat")
        last_tick = await r_cmd.get("last_tick_ist") or "N/A"
        disk_free_gb = shutil.disk_usage(project_root).free / (1024 ** 3)
        hb_info = json.loads(hb_raw) if hb_raw else {}

        msg = (
            f"💓 <b>Live System Heartbeat</b>\n"
            f"━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n"
            f"• <b>Engine Mode:</b> <code>{hb_info.get('mode', 'IDLE')}</code>\n"
            f"• <b>Ticks Processed:</b> <code>{hb_info.get('ticks_processed', 0):,}</code>\n"
            f"• <b>Active Micro-Bots:</b> <code>{hb_info.get('active_bots', 0)}</code>\n"
            f"• <b>Last Tick Time:</b> <code>{last_tick} IST</code>\n"
            f"• <b>Heartbeat Wall Time:</b> <code>{hb_info.get('wall_time', 'N/A')}</code>\n"
            f"• <b>Free SSD Disk:</b> <code>{disk_free_gb:.2f} GB</code>"
        )
        await update.message.reply_text(msg, parse_mode="HTML")
    finally:
        await r_cmd.aclose()


async def bots_command(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    profiles = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(bots_dir, "profile_*.json")))
    if not profiles:
        await update.message.reply_text("⚠️ No StockMicroBot profiles found in models/bots/.")
        return
    lines = [f"🧠 <b>Calibrated StockMicroBots ({len(profiles)} Active)</b>\n"]
    for p_file in profiles[:25]:
        try:
            with open(p_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                p = json.load(f)
            sym = p.get("symbol", "").replace("-EQ", "")
            ml_badge = "🤖ML" if p.get("has_ml_model") else "📐STAT"
            lines.append(
                f"• <code>{sym:<10}</code> ({ml_badge}) | CVD:<code>{p.get('cvd_threshold')}</code> "
                f"| SL:<code>{p.get('sl_pct', 0)*100:.1f}%</code> | AI:<code>{p.get('ai_score', 0.5):.2f}</code>"
            )
        except Exception:
            pass
    await update.message.reply_text("\n".join(lines), parse_mode="HTML")


async def quota_command(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    await update.message.reply_text(APITracker.get_summary_text(), parse_mode="HTML")


async def eod_command(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    rep = TelegramReporter()
    await update.message.reply_text(rep.format_eod_summary_text(), parse_mode="HTML")


async def screener_command(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    await update.message.reply_text("🔍 Launching EOD Ensemble Screener in background...")
    subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "src/scripts/screener.py"], cwd=project_root)


async def restart_command(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    if sys.platform == "win32":
        await update.message.reply_text("ℹ️ /restart is for Linux/Azure tmux deployment.")
        return
    await update.message.reply_text("🔄 Executing restart_ws.sh (Updating symbols, bots & 3-pane tmux)...")
    subprocess.Popen(["bash", "restart_ws.sh"], cwd=project_root)


async def kill_command(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    r_cmd = get_redis_client()
    try:
        await r_cmd.set("EMERGENCY_STOP", "1")
        await update.message.reply_text(
            "🚨 <b>EMERGENCY OVERRIDE ENGAGED</b>\nKill flag sent. Engine will square off immediately.",
            parse_mode="HTML",
        )
    finally:
        await r_cmd.aclose()


async def log_command(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    today_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    log_path = os.path.join(base_dir, "logs", f"signals_{today_str}.log")
    if os.path.exists(log_path):
        with open(log_path, "rb") as doc:
            await update.message.reply_document(document=doc)
    else:
        await update.message.reply_text(f"⚠️ No log file generated yet for {today_str}.")


async def csv_command(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    today_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    csv_path = os.path.join(base_dir, f"paper_trades_{today_str}.csv")
    if os.path.exists(csv_path):
        with open(csv_path, "rb") as doc:
            await update.message.reply_document(document=doc)
    else:
        await update.message.reply_text("⚠️ No CSV trades recorded today.")


async def send_telegram_alert(session, chat_id, text):
    url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{TOKEN}/sendMessage"
    payload = {"chat_id": chat_id, "text": text, "parse_mode": "HTML", "disable_web_page_preview": True}
    try:
        async with session.post(url, json=payload, timeout=10) as response:
            if response.status == 429:
                retry_after = (await response.json()).get("parameters", {}).get("retry_after", 1.0)
                await asyncio.sleep(retry_after)
                return await send_telegram_alert(session, chat_id, text)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[NETWORK ERROR] Failed to push alert: {e}")


async def run_dispatcher():
    print("[DISPATCHER] Live Push Notification Daemon Active...")
    r_disp = get_redis_client()
    await r_disp.set("live_balance", get_disk_state().get("current_balance", 100000.0))

    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        while True:
            try:
                result = await r_disp.blpop("sys_alerts_queue", timeout=5)
                if not result:
                    continue
                _, data_str = result
                alert = json.loads(data_str)
                tier = int(alert.get("tier", 3))
                chat_id = TIERS.get(tier)
                if chat_id:
                    icons = {1: "🚨", 2: "⚡", 3: "⚙️"}
                    msg = f"{icons.get(tier, '🔔')} <b>[{alert.get('source', 'SYS')}]</b>\n{alert.get('message', '')}"
                    await send_telegram_alert(session, chat_id, msg)
                await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[DISPATCHER WARN] Reconnecting Redis queue: {e}")
                await asyncio.sleep(3)
                r_disp = get_redis_client()


async def main():
    print("[SYSTEM] Booting Unified Telegram C2 Node...")
    app = ApplicationBuilder().token(TOKEN).build()

    app.add_handler(CommandHandler("start", start_command))
    app.add_handler(CommandHandler("dashboard", dashboard_command))
    app.add_handler(CommandHandler("status", status_command))
    app.add_handler(CommandHandler("bots", bots_command))
    app.add_handler(CommandHandler("quota", quota_command))
    app.add_handler(CommandHandler("eod", eod_command))
    app.add_handler(CommandHandler("screener", screener_command))
    app.add_handler(CommandHandler("restart", restart_command))
    app.add_handler(CommandHandler("kill", kill_command))
    app.add_handler(CommandHandler("logs", log_command))
    app.add_handler(CommandHandler("trades", csv_command))

    await app.initialize()
    await app.start()
    await app.updater.start_polling(drop_pending_updates=True)
    await run_dispatcher()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if not TOKEN:
        print("[FATAL] TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN missing in .env")
        sys.exit(1)
    asyncio.run(main())

import os
import csv
import json
import requests
import subprocess
from datetime import datetime
from dotenv import load_dotenv

class TelegramReporter:
    def __init__(self):
        load_dotenv()
        self.bot_token = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN")
        self.chat_id = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID")
        self.base_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(__file__)), "trading")
        self.today_str = datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')
        self.csv_path = os.path.join(self.base_dir, f"paper_trades_{self.today_str}.csv")
        self.portfolio_path = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "portfolio_state.json")
        self.autopsy_path = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "logs", f"trade_autopsy_{self.today_str}.xlsx")

    def get_portfolio_state(self) -> dict:
        default = {
            "current_balance": 100000.0,
            "initial_balance": 100000.0,
            "daily_pnl": 0.0,
            "peak_capital_deployed": 0.0,
            "total_turnover_deployed": 0.0,
            "max_concurrent_positions": int(os.getenv("MAX_CONCURRENT_POSITIONS", 4))
        }
        if os.path.exists(self.portfolio_path):
            try:
                with open(self.portfolio_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    default.update(json.load(f))
            except Exception:
                pass
        return default

    def get_final_balance(self) -> float:
        return float(self.get_portfolio_state().get("current_balance", 100000.0))

    def parse_daily_metrics(self) -> dict:
        metrics = {
            "total_orders": 0, "closed_trades": 0, "wins": 0, "losses": 0,
            "gross_pnl": 0.0, "total_taxes": 0.0, "net_pnl": 0.0,
            "per_stock": {}, "entry_sources": {}
        }
        if not os.path.exists(self.csv_path):
            return metrics

        with open(self.csv_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            reader = csv.DictReader(f)
            for row in reader:
                metrics["total_orders"] += 1
                sym = (row.get('symbol') or f"TOK_{row.get('security_id')}").replace("-EQ", "")
                gross = float(row.get('gross_pnl', 0.0))
                taxes = float(row.get('transaction_taxes', 0.0))
                net = float(row.get('net_pnl', 0.0))
                src = row.get('trigger_source', 'SYSTEM') or 'SYSTEM'

                metrics["total_taxes"] += taxes
                metrics["gross_pnl"] += gross
                metrics["net_pnl"] += net

                st = metrics["per_stock"].setdefault(sym, {"trades": 0, "gross": 0.0, "taxes": 0.0, "net": 0.0, "source": src})
                st["gross"] += gross
                st["taxes"] += taxes
                st["net"] += net

                if gross == 0.0:
                    # Entry order leg: record which brain triggered the entry
                    st["source"] = src
                    metrics["entry_sources"][src] = metrics["entry_sources"].get(src, 0) + 1
                else:
                    # Exit order leg
                    metrics["closed_trades"] += 1
                    st["trades"] += 1
                    if net > 0:
                        metrics["wins"] += 1
                    else:
                        metrics["losses"] += 1
        return metrics

    def format_eod_summary_text(self, mode_label: str = "LIVE") -> str:
        m = self.parse_daily_metrics()
        state = self.get_portfolio_state()
        init_bal = float(state.get("initial_balance", 100000.0))
        final_bal = float(state.get("current_balance", 100000.0))
        peak_notional = float(state.get("peak_capital_deployed", 0.0))
        peak_margin_cash = peak_notional / 5.0
        min_idle_cash = max(0.0, init_bal - peak_margin_cash)

        net_pnl = final_bal - init_bal
        roi_total_pct = (net_pnl / init_bal * 100.0) if init_bal > 0 else 0.0
        roi_active_pct = (net_pnl / peak_notional * 100.0) if peak_notional > 0 else 0.0
        win_rate = (m['wins'] / m['closed_trades'] * 100.0) if m['closed_trades'] > 0 else 0.0
        pnl_icon = "🟢" if net_pnl >= 0 else "🔴"

        lines = [
            f"📊 <b>END-OF-DAY TRADING REPORT ({mode_label})</b>",
            f"📅 Date: <code>{datetime.now().strftime('%d-%b-%Y')}</code>",
            "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━",
            "💰 <b>1. PROFIT & LOSS SUMMARY</b>",
            f"• Starting Balance: <b>₹{init_bal:,.2f}</b>",
            f"• Ending Balance: <b>₹{final_bal:,.2f}</b>",
            f"• Market Profit (Before Tax): <b>₹{m['gross_pnl']:+,.2f}</b>",
            f"• Brokerage & Govt Taxes: <b>-₹{m['total_taxes']:,.2f}</b>",
            f"• {pnl_icon} <b>Net Take-Home P&L: ₹{net_pnl:+,.2f} ({roi_total_pct:+.2f}%)</b>",
            "",
            "🏦 <b>2. CAPITAL USAGE (ACTIVE vs IDLE)</b>",
            f"• Max Stock Value Held at Once: <b>₹{peak_notional:,.0f}</b>",
            f"• Cash Margin Used (5x Leverage): <b>₹{peak_margin_cash:,.0f}</b>",
            f"• Idle Cash Left Untouched: <b>₹{min_idle_cash:,.0f}</b>",
            f"• Return % on Active Stock Value: <b>{roi_active_pct:+.2f}%</b>",
            "",
            "🎯 <b>3. WIN RATE & ACCURACY</b>",
            f"• Completed Trades: <b>{m['closed_trades']}</b> (Total Orders: {m['total_orders']})",
            f"• Winning Trades: <b>{m['wins']}</b> ✅ | Losing Trades: <b>{m['losses']}</b> ❌",
            f"• Win Accuracy: <b>{win_rate:.1f}%</b>",
        ]

        if m["entry_sources"]:
            lines.append("")
            lines.append("🧠 <b>4. WHO TRIGGERED TODAY'S TRADES?</b>")
            for src, cnt in m["entry_sources"].items():
                lines.append(f"• <code>{src}</code>: <b>{cnt} entries</b>")

        if m["per_stock"]:
            lines.append("")
            lines.append("📈 <b>5. STOCK-BY-STOCK P&L (NET AFTER TAX)</b>")
            sorted_stocks = sorted(m["per_stock"].items(), key=lambda x: x[1]["net"], reverse=True)
            for sym, st in sorted_stocks:
                ico = "🟢" if st["net"] >= 0 else "🔴"
                lines.append(
                    f"{ico} <code>{sym:<10}</code>: <b>₹{st['net']:+7.2f}</b> "
                    f"(Gross: ₹{st['gross']:+.0f} | {st['trades']}T | {st['source']})"
                )

        return "\n".join(lines)

    def send_report(self, mode_label: str = "LIVE"):
        message = self.format_eod_summary_text(mode_label=mode_label)
        if not self.bot_token or not self.chat_id:
            return
        url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{self.bot_token}/sendMessage"
        try:
            requests.post(url, json={"chat_id": self.chat_id, "text": message, "parse_mode": "HTML"}, timeout=10)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[TELEGRAM WARN] Report dispatch failed: {e}")

    def send_file(self, file_path: str):
        if not file_path or not os.path.exists(file_path) or not self.bot_token:
            return
        url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{self.bot_token}/sendDocument"
        try:
            with open(file_path, 'rb') as document:
                requests.post(url, data={"chat_id": self.chat_id}, files={'document': document}, timeout=25)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def backup_postgres(self) -> str:
        db_user = os.getenv("DB_USER")
        db_pass = os.getenv("DB_PASS")
        db_name = os.getenv("DB_NAME")
        db_host = os.getenv("DB_HOST", "localhost")
        if not all([db_user, db_pass, db_name]):
            return None
        dump_filename = f"{db_name}_backup_{self.today_str}.sql.gz"
        dump_path = os.path.join(self.base_dir, dump_filename)
        env = os.environ.copy()
        env["PGPASSWORD"] = db_pass
        command = f"pg_dump -h {db_host} -U {db_user} -d {db_name} | gzip > {dump_path}"
        try:
            subprocess.run(command, shell=True, env=env, check=True)
            return dump_path
        except Exception:
            return None

    def send_premarket_watchlist(self, watchlist_path: str = None):
        path = watchlist_path or os.path.join(os.path.dirname(self.base_dir), "..", "target_ticks.json")
        if not os.path.exists(path) or not self.bot_token:
            return
        try:
            with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                items = json.load(f)
            lines = [f"🚀 <b>Pre-Market Target Watchlist ({len(items)} Stocks)</b>\n"]
            for i, item in enumerate(items[:15], 1):
                sym = item.get("symbol", "")
                score = float(item.get("final_score", item.get("master_score", 0.0)))
                ai_s = float(item.get("ai_score", 0.50))
                cohort = item.get("cohort", "MATH")
                lines.append(f"{i}. <code>{sym:<12}</code> | Final: <b>{score:.3f}</b> (AI: {ai_s:.2f}) | {cohort}")
            url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{self.bot_token}/sendMessage"
            requests.post(url, json={"chat_id": self.chat_id, "text": "\n".join(lines), "parse_mode": "HTML"}, timeout=10)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[TELEGRAM WARN] Watchlist dispatch failed: {e}")

    def send_ai_catalyst_briefing(self, watchlist_path: str = None):
        self.send_premarket_watchlist(watchlist_path)

    def dispatch(self):
        self.send_premarket_watchlist()

import csv
import os
import logging
from datetime import datetime

class AuditLogger:
    def __init__(self):
        self.filepath = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), f"paper_trades_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}.csv")
        if not os.path.exists(self.filepath):
            with open(self.filepath, 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                csv.writer(f).writerow([
                    'trace_id', 'tick_timestamp', 'exec_wall_time', 'security_id', 'symbol',
                    'action', 'fill_price', 'qty', 'notional_value', 'trigger_source', 'trigger_reason',
                    'latency_ms', 'slippage', 'gross_pnl', 'transaction_taxes', 'net_pnl', 'total_balance'
                ])

    def log_trade(
        self, trace_id, tick_time, sec_id, action, price, qty, latency, slippage,
        gross_pnl, taxes, net_pnl, balance, symbol="", trigger_source="", trigger_reason=""
    ):
        with open(self.filepath, 'a', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            csv.writer(f).writerow([
                trace_id, tick_time, datetime.now().isoformat(), sec_id, symbol or f"TOK_{sec_id}",
                action, round(price, 2), qty, round(price * qty, 2),
                trigger_source or "SYSTEM", trigger_reason or "STANDARD",
                round(latency, 2), round(slippage, 4),
                round(gross_pnl, 2), round(taxes, 2), round(net_pnl, 2), round(balance, 2)
            ])

def setup_signal_logger():
    logger = logging.getLogger("SignalLogger")
    logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
    logger.propagate = False

    log_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "logs")
    os.makedirs(log_dir, exist_ok=True)
    filepath = os.path.join(log_dir, f"signals_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}.log")

    if not logger.handlers:
        fh = logging.FileHandler(filepath, encoding='utf-8')
        fh.setLevel(logging.INFO)
        formatter = logging.Formatter('%(asctime)s | %(message)s', datefmt='%H:%M:%S')
        fh.setFormatter(formatter)
        logger.addHandler(fh)

    return logger

import os
import pandas as pd
from datetime import datetime

def export_autopsy_to_excel(autopsy_records: list, base_dir: str = None) -> str:
    """
    Takes in-memory autopsy records, sorts them by security_id and timestamp,
    and exports a formatted Excel file for post-trade analysis.
    """
    if not autopsy_records:
        print("[AUTOPSY] No trade attempts or signals were generated today. Skipping export.")
        return ""

    # Ensure output is placed into src/trading/logs
    if not base_dir:
        base_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

    logs_dir = os.path.join(base_dir, "logs")
    os.makedirs(logs_dir, exist_ok=True)

    df = pd.DataFrame(autopsy_records)

    # 1. Chronological and Token Sort
    df.sort_values(by=["security_id", "timestamp_ist"], ascending=[True, True], inplace=True)

    date_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    output_path = os.path.join(logs_dir, f"trade_autopsy_{date_str}.xlsx")

    # 2. Write to Excel with Clean Column Sizing
    with pd.ExcelWriter(output_path, engine="openpyxl") as writer:
        df.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name="Trade_Autopsy")
        worksheet = writer.sheets["Trade_Autopsy"]

        for col in worksheet.columns:
            max_len = max(len(str(cell.value or "")) for cell in col)
            col_letter = col[0].column_letter
            worksheet.column_dimensions[col_letter].width = max(max_len + 3, 12)

    print(f"\n[AUTOPSY SUCCESS] Exported {len(df)} signal traces to: {output_path}")
    return output_path

import os
import uuid
import time
import requests
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from src.utils.api_tracker import APITracker, get_dhan_token

load_dotenv()

class DhanSandboxClient:
    """
    Dhan Sandbox / Live Order Router using DHAN_CLIENT_ID, DHAN_PIN, DHAN_TOTP_SECRET, DHAN_API_KEY.
    Controlled via EXECUTION_MODE in .env:
      - PAPER        : Local paper simulation only (default)
      - DHAN_SANDBOX : Fires orders to Dhan Sandbox endpoint + paper ledger
      - DUAL         : Fires both Dhan Sandbox & Paper ledger simultaneously
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.mode = os.getenv("EXECUTION_MODE", "PAPER").strip().upper()
        self.client_id = os.getenv("DHAN_CLIENT_ID", "")
        self.pin = os.getenv("DHAN_PIN", "")
        self.totp_secret = os.getenv("DHAN_TOTP_SECRET", "")
        self.api_key = os.getenv("DHAN_API_KEY", "")
        self.base_url = os.getenv("DHAN_SANDBOX_URL", "https://sandbox.dhan.co/v2")

    def is_enabled(self) -> bool:
        return self.mode in ("DHAN_SANDBOX", "DUAL", "SANDBOX")

    def place_intraday_order(self, sec_id: str, symbol: str, action: str, qty: int, price: float, trace_id: str) -> dict:
        t0 = time.time()
        payload = {
            "dhanClientId": self.client_id or "SANDBOX",
            "correlationId": trace_id[:24],
            "transactionType": "BUY" if action == "BUY" else "SELL",
            "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
            "productType": "INTRADAY",
            "orderType": "LIMIT",
            "validity": "DAY",
            "tradingSymbol": symbol.replace("-EQ", ""),
            "securityId": str(sec_id),
            "quantity": int(qty),
            "price": round(float(price), 2)
        }

        token = get_dhan_token()
        if not token:
            return {
                "status": "SANDBOX_SIMULATED",
                "order_id": f"DHAN-SBX-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8].upper()}",
                "latency_ms": round((time.time() - t0) * 1000, 2),
                "payload": payload
            }

        try:
            headers = {
                "access-token": token,
                "client-id": self.client_id,
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                "Accept": "application/json"
            }
            resp = requests.post(f"{self.base_url}/orders", json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=5)
            APITracker.record("DHAN", success=(resp.status_code in (200, 201)), rate_limited=(resp.status_code == 429))
            latency_ms = round((time.time() - t0) * 1000, 2)
            if resp.status_code in (200, 201):
                data = resp.json()
                return {
                    "status": "SANDBOX_FILLED",
                    "order_id": data.get("orderId", f"DHAN-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"),
                    "latency_ms": latency_ms
                }
            return {
                "status": f"SANDBOX_HTTP_{resp.status_code}",
                "order_id": f"DHAN-SBX-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}",
                "latency_ms": latency_ms
            }
        except Exception as e:
            APITracker.record("DHAN", success=False)
            return {
                "status": f"SANDBOX_ERR:{str(e)[:40]}",
                "order_id": f"DHAN-LOCAL-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}",
                "latency_ms": round((time.time() - t0) * 1000, 2)
            }

FILE: .\src\trading\execution_client.py
============================================================
from src.trading.portfolio import PortfolioManager
from src.trading.audit_logger import AuditLogger
from src.trading.dhan_sandbox import DhanSandboxClient

class ExecutionEngine:
    def __init__(self, portfolio: PortfolioManager, logger: AuditLogger):
        self.portfolio = portfolio
        self.logger = logger
        self.simulated_latency = 0.0
        self.dhan_sandbox = DhanSandboxClient()

    def _calculate_indian_taxes(self, action: str, qty: int, price: float) -> float:
        turnover = float(qty * price)
        calculated_brokerage = turnover * 0.001
        brokerage = max(5.0, min(20.0, calculated_brokerage))
        exchange_txn_charge = turnover * 0.000030699
        sebi_fee = turnover * 0.000001
        gst = (brokerage + exchange_txn_charge + sebi_fee) * 0.18
        stt = (turnover * 0.00025) if action == 'SELL' else 0.0
        stamp_duty = (turnover * 0.00003) if action == 'BUY' else 0.0
        return round(brokerage + exchange_txn_charge + sebi_fee + gst + stt + stamp_duty, 2)

    def execute_paper_trade(
        self, tick: dict, action: str, qty: int, trace_id: str = "NO_TRACE",
        bypass_margin: bool = False, symbol: str = "", trigger_source: str = "", trigger_reason: str = ""
    ) -> str:
        sec_id = str(tick.get('security_id'))
        tick_timestamp = tick.get('timestamp')

        maker_price = (
            tick.get('bid') if action == 'BUY' else tick.get('ask')
        ) or tick.get('ltp') or tick.get('price') or 0.0

        if not maker_price or maker_price <= 0:
            return "REJECTED_INVALID_PRICE"

        notional_required = float(maker_price) * qty
        current_pos = self.portfolio.positions.get(sec_id)
        is_closing_trade = current_pos is not None and current_pos['side'] != action

        if not bypass_margin and not is_closing_trade:
            allowed, reject_reason = self.portfolio.can_take_trade(sec_id, action, notional_required)
            if not allowed:
                return reject_reason

        fill_price = float(maker_price)
        latency_ms = self.simulated_latency * 1000.0

        if self.dhan_sandbox.is_enabled():
            sbx_res = self.dhan_sandbox.place_intraday_order(
                sec_id=sec_id, symbol=symbol or f"TOK_{sec_id}",
                action=action, qty=qty, price=fill_price, trace_id=trace_id
            )
            latency_ms = sbx_res.get("latency_ms", latency_ms)
            trigger_reason = f"{trigger_reason} | {sbx_res.get('order_id', '')}"

        transaction_taxes = self._calculate_indian_taxes(action, qty, fill_price)
        gross_booked_pnl = self.portfolio.update_position(
            sec_id, action, qty, fill_price, symbol=symbol, trigger_source=trigger_source
        )
        self.portfolio.current_balance -= transaction_taxes
        net_booked_pnl = gross_booked_pnl - transaction_taxes

        self.logger.log_trade(
            trace_id, tick_timestamp, sec_id, action, fill_price, qty,
            latency_ms, 0.0,
            gross_booked_pnl, transaction_taxes, net_booked_pnl, self.portfolio.current_balance,
            symbol=symbol, trigger_source=trigger_source, trigger_reason=trigger_reason
        )
        return "EXECUTED"

============================================================
FILE: .\src\trading\portfolio.py
============================================================
import json
import os

class PortfolioManager:
    def __init__(
        self,
        starting_balance: float = 100000.0,
        max_allocation_pct: float = 0.20,
        max_loss_pct: float = 0.02,
        max_concurrent_positions: int = None,
        state_file: str = "portfolio_state.json"
    ):
        self.state_file = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), state_file)
        loaded_balance = self._load_state(starting_balance)

        self.initial_balance = loaded_balance
        self.current_balance = loaded_balance
        self.max_allocation_pct = max_allocation_pct
        self.max_loss_pct = max_loss_pct
        self.max_concurrent_positions = int(
            max_concurrent_positions or os.getenv("MAX_CONCURRENT_POSITIONS", 4)
        )

        self.positions = {}
        self.daily_pnl = 0.0
        self.peak_capital_deployed = 0.0
        self.total_turnover_deployed = 0.0

    def _load_state(self, default_balance: float) -> float:
        if os.path.exists(self.state_file):
            try:
                with open(self.state_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    return float(data.get("current_balance", default_balance))
            except Exception:
                pass
        return default_balance

    def save_state(self):
        with open(self.state_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump({
                "current_balance": round(self.current_balance, 2),
                "initial_balance": round(self.initial_balance, 2),
                "daily_pnl": round(self.daily_pnl, 2),
                "peak_capital_deployed": round(self.peak_capital_deployed, 2),
                "total_turnover_deployed": round(self.total_turnover_deployed, 2),
                "max_concurrent_positions": self.max_concurrent_positions
            }, f, indent=4)

    def get_active_notional(self) -> float:
        return sum(pos['qty'] * pos['avg_price'] for pos in self.positions.values())

    def get_margin_used(self) -> float:
        return self.get_active_notional() / 5.0

    def get_idle_capital(self) -> float:
        return max(0.0, self.current_balance - self.get_margin_used())

    def can_take_trade(self, sec_id, action: str, requested_notional: float) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        sec_id = str(sec_id)
        if self.daily_pnl <= -(self.initial_balance * self.max_loss_pct):
            return False, "REJECTED_MAX_DAILY_DRAWDOWN"

        if sec_id not in self.positions and len(self.positions) >= self.max_concurrent_positions:
            return False, f"REJECTED_MAX_SLOTS_{self.max_concurrent_positions}"

        # Exact live portfolio limit: single position notional <= current_balance * max_allocation_pct (20%)
        if requested_notional > (self.current_balance * self.max_allocation_pct):
            return False, "REJECTED_PORTFOLIO_LIMITS"

        if (requested_notional / 5.0) > self.get_idle_capital():
            return False, "REJECTED_INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS"

        return True, "OK"

    def update_position(self, sec_id, action: str, qty: int, fill_price: float, symbol: str = "", trigger_source: str = "") -> float:
        sec_id = str(sec_id)
        pos = self.positions.get(sec_id)
        realized_pnl = 0.0
        trade_notional = float(qty * fill_price)

        if not pos:
            self.positions[sec_id] = {
                'side': action,
                'qty': qty,
                'avg_price': fill_price,
                'symbol': symbol or f"TOK_{sec_id}",
                'trigger_source': trigger_source or "BOT_MATH"
            }
            self.total_turnover_deployed += trade_notional
        elif pos['side'] == action:
            total_qty = pos['qty'] + qty
            pos['avg_price'] = ((pos['avg_price'] * pos['qty']) + (fill_price * qty)) / total_qty
            pos['qty'] = total_qty
            self.total_turnover_deployed += trade_notional
        else:
            close_qty = min(pos['qty'], qty)
            if pos['side'] == 'BUY':
                realized_pnl = (fill_price - pos['avg_price']) * close_qty
            else:
                realized_pnl = (pos['avg_price'] - fill_price) * close_qty

            self.daily_pnl += realized_pnl
            self.current_balance += realized_pnl

            if pos['qty'] == close_qty:
                del self.positions[sec_id]
            else:
                pos['qty'] -= close_qty

        current_notional = self.get_active_notional()
        if current_notional > self.peak_capital_deployed:
            self.peak_capital_deployed = current_notional

        return realized_pnl

FILE: .\src\trading\risk_manager.py
============================================================
class RiskManager:
    def __init__(self, base_risk_pct: float = 0.10):
        # The absolute maximum percentage of free cash you will risk on a 1.0 (Perfect) setup
        self.base_risk_pct = base_risk_pct

    def calculate_confidence(self, obi: float, cvd: float, spread: float) -> float:
        """Converts raw indicators into a 0.0 to 1.0 Confidence Score."""
        # 1. Normalize OBI (Max strength is 1.0)
        obi_strength = abs(obi)
        
        # 2. Normalize CVD (Assuming 2000 delta is a 'maximum' benchmark)
        cvd_strength = min(abs(cvd) / 2000.0, 1.0)
        
        # 3. Spread Penalty (tiered: tight spread = full confidence, wide = reduced)
        if spread < 0.002:      # < 0.2% spread — excellent liquidity
            spread_penalty = 1.0
        elif spread < 0.005:    # 0.2% - 0.5% — acceptable
            spread_penalty = 0.8
        elif spread < 0.01:     # 0.5% - 1.0% — cautious
            spread_penalty = 0.5
        else:                   # > 1.0% — illiquid, heavily penalize
            spread_penalty = 0.2
        
        # Weighting: 60% Order Book, 40% Volume Delta
        confidence = ((obi_strength * 0.6) + (cvd_strength * 0.4)) * spread_penalty
        
        # Cap at 1.0 just to be mathematically safe
        return min(confidence, 1.0)

    def calculate_position_size(self, confidence: float, free_cash: float, ltp: float, available_vol: int) -> int:
        """Calculates dynamic quantity based on confidence, portfolio heat, and order book liquidity."""
        
        # Reject weak setups outright (e.g., Confidence below 30%)
        if confidence < 0.30:
            return 0

        # Calculate how much cash we are allowed to use
        target_margin = free_cash * self.base_risk_pct * confidence
        
        # Calculate theoretical shares
        if ltp == 0:
            return 0
        theoretical_qty = int(target_margin // ltp)
        
        # Liquidity Cap: Never buy more than what is available at the best price level
        final_qty = min(theoretical_qty, available_vol)
        
        return final_qty

"""
src/trading/strategy_engine.py
Exact Live Winning Math + Per-Stock Micro-Bot Authority + Max Concurrent Slot Control.

import sys

import uuid

import lightgbm as lgb

from dotenv import load_dotenv

from src.trading.indicators.obi import OrderBookImbalance
