BOTS_DIR = os.path.join(project_root, "models", "bots")
# Exact live excluded macro indices & anchor components
EXCLUDED_TOKENS = {"99926000", "99926009", "99926002", "26000", "26009", "26014", "2885", "1333"}


class StockMicroBot:
    """Per-stock autonomous brain holding custom LightGBM model, 2m bar aggregator, and metadata."""
    def __init__(self, sec_id: str):
        self.sec_id = str(sec_id)
        self.profile = self._load_profile()
        self.symbol = self.profile.get("symbol", f"TOK_{self.sec_id}")
        self.model = self._load_model()
        self.bars = deque(self.profile.get("warmup_bars", []), maxlen=30)
        self.current_bucket = None
        self.active_bar = None
        self.last_ml_eval = {
            "regime": None,
            "prob_bull": 0.33,
            "prob_bear": 0.33,
            "prob_chop": 0.34,
            "edge": 0.0
        }
        if self.model and len(self.bars) >= 9:
            last_c = float(self.bars[-1]["close"])
            self._evaluate_ml(last_c, last_c * float(self.profile.get("atr_2m_pct", 0.0025)))

    def _load_profile(self) -> dict:
        p_path = os.path.join(BOTS_DIR, f"profile_{self.sec_id}.json")
        if os.path.exists(p_path):
            try:
                with open(p_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    return json.load(f)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return {
            "token": self.sec_id,
            "symbol": f"TOK_{self.sec_id}",
            "cohort": "MATH",
            "ai_score": 0.50,
            "master_score": 0.60,
            "direction_bias": "NEUTRAL",
            "has_ml_model": False
        }

    def _load_model(self):
        m_path = os.path.join(BOTS_DIR, f"bot_{self.sec_id}.txt")
        if os.path.exists(m_path) and os.path.getsize(m_path) > 500:
            try:
                return lgb.Booster(model_file=m_path, params={"num_threads": 1})
            except Exception:
                try:
                    os.remove(m_path)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        return None

    def on_tick(self, tick_ts_ms: int, ltp: float, ltq: int, vwap_val: float, atr_val: float):
        if ltp <= 0:
            return
        bucket = (tick_ts_ms // 1000) // 120 * 120
        if self.current_bucket is None:
            self.current_bucket = bucket
            self.active_bar = {"open": ltp, "high": ltp, "low": ltp, "close": ltp, "vol": max(1, ltq)}
            return
        if bucket > self.current_bucket:
            self.bars.append(self.active_bar)
            self._evaluate_ml(vwap_val, atr_val)
            self.current_bucket = bucket
            self.active_bar = {"open": ltp, "high": ltp, "low": ltp, "close": ltp, "vol": max(1, ltq)}
        else:
            if ltp > self.active_bar["high"]:
                self.active_bar["high"] = ltp
            if ltp < self.active_bar["low"]:
                self.active_bar["low"] = ltp
            self.active_bar["close"] = ltp
            self.active_bar["vol"] += max(0, ltq)

    def _evaluate_ml(self, vwap_val: float, atr_val: float):
        if not self.model or len(self.bars) < 9:
            return
        try:
            closes = [b["close"] for b in self.bars]
            vols = [b["vol"] for b in self.bars]
            cur = self.bars[-1]
            c_now = cur["close"]
            ret_2m = (c_now / closes[-2]) - 1.0 if closes[-2] else 0.0
            ret_6m = (c_now / closes[-4]) - 1.0 if len(closes) >= 4 and closes[-4] else 0.0
            ret_16m = (c_now / closes[-9]) - 1.0 if len(closes) >= 9 and closes[-9] else 0.0
            eff_atr = atr_val if atr_val > 0 else (c_now * self.profile.get("atr_2m_pct", 0.0025))
            vwap_dist_atr = float(np.clip((c_now - (vwap_val or c_now)) / max(eff_atr, 1e-4), -5.0, 5.0))
            rvol_2m = float(np.clip(cur["vol"] / max(np.mean(vols[-20:]), 1.0), 0.0, 10.0))
            rng = max(cur["high"] - cur["low"], 1e-4)
            body_ratio = float(np.clip((cur["close"] - cur["open"]) / rng, -1.0, 1.0))
            atr_pct = float(np.clip(eff_atr / max(c_now, 1.0), 0.0005, 0.05))

            diffs = np.diff(closes[-15:])
            gains = np.mean(np.clip(diffs, 0, None))
            losses = max(np.mean(np.clip(-diffs, 0, None)), 1e-6)
            rsi_14 = float(np.clip(1.0 - (1.0 / (1.0 + (gains / losses))), 0.0, 1.0))

            X = np.array([[ret_2m, ret_6m, ret_16m, vwap_dist_atr, rvol_2m, body_ratio, atr_pct, rsi_14]], dtype=np.float32)
            probs = self.model.predict(X)[0]
            p_bear, p_chop, p_bull = float(probs[0]), float(probs[1]), float(probs[2])

            if p_chop >= 0.45:
                reg = 0
            elif (p_bull - p_bear) >= 0.05:
                reg = 1
            elif (p_bear - p_bull) >= 0.05:
                reg = -1
            else:
                reg = 0

            self.last_ml_eval = {
                "regime": reg,
                "prob_bull": p_bull,
                "prob_bear": p_bear,
                "prob_chop": p_chop,
                "edge": abs(p_bull - p_bear)
            }
        except Exception:
            pass

    def evaluate_signal_authority(self, proposed_action: str) -> tuple[bool, str, str]:
        ml_reg = self.last_ml_eval.get("regime")
        if ml_reg is not None:
            p_bull = float(self.last_ml_eval.get("prob_bull", 0.33))
            p_bear = float(self.last_ml_eval.get("prob_bear", 0.33))
            if proposed_action == "BUY" and ml_reg == -1 and (p_bear - p_bull) > 0.15:
                return False, "VETO_BOT_BEAR", f"BOT_BEAR({p_bear*100:.0f}%)"
            if proposed_action == "SELL" and ml_reg == 1 and (p_bull - p_bear) > 0.15:
                return False, "VETO_BOT_BULL", f"BOT_BULL({p_bull*100:.0f}%)"
            reg_tag = "BOT_BULL" if p_bull >= p_bear else "BOT_BEAR"
            return True, "BOT_ML_MODEL", f"{reg_tag}({max(p_bull, p_bear)*100:.0f}%)"

        return True, "STOCK_ORDERFLOW_MATH", "PURE_STOCK_MATH"


def sync_live_redis_state(
    r: redis.Redis, portfolio: PortfolioManager, last_tick_str: str = "",
    processed: int = 0, active_bots: int = 0, mode: str = "LIVE"
):
    r.set("live_balance", round(portfolio.current_balance, 2))
    r.set("live_positions", json.dumps(portfolio.positions))
    r.set("live_active_capital", round(portfolio.get_active_notional(), 2))
    r.set("live_idle_capital", round(portfolio.get_idle_capital(), 2))
    if last_tick_str:
        r.set("last_tick_ist", last_tick_str)
    r.set("engine_heartbeat", json.dumps({
        "mode": mode,
        "ticks_processed": processed,
        "active_bots": active_bots,
        "open_positions": len(portfolio.positions),
        "wall_time": datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
    }))


def run_end_of_day_reporting(portfolio: PortfolioManager, autopsy_records=None, replay_mode=False):
    portfolio.save_state()
    print(f"[STATE SAVED] Balance: Rs.{portfolio.current_balance:,.2f} | Peak Active Capital: Rs.{portfolio.peak_capital_deployed:,.2f}")

    excel_path = None
    if autopsy_records:
        try:
            excel_path = export_autopsy_to_excel(autopsy_records)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[WARNING] Autopsy Excel export failed: {e}")

    try:
        mode_tag = "REPLAY" if replay_mode else "LIVE"
        print(f"[SYSTEM] Dispatching {mode_tag} End-of-Day Telegram Report...")
        reporter = TelegramReporter()
        reporter.send_report(mode_label=mode_tag)
        reporter.send_file(reporter.csv_path)
        if not replay_mode:
            log_path = os.path.join(reporter.base_dir, "logs", f"signals_{reporter.today_str}.log")
            reporter.send_file(log_path)
            if excel_path and os.path.exists(excel_path):
                reporter.send_file(excel_path)
            db_dump = reporter.backup_postgres()
            if db_dump:
                reporter.send_file(db_dump)
        print("[SYSTEM] Telegram dispatch complete.")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[WARNING] Telegram reporting failed: {e}")

    if not replay_mode:
        try:
            print("\n[SYSTEM] Commencing Cloud Handoff to AWS S3...")
            AWSSync().upload_daily_logs()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[WARNING] AWS sync failed: {e}")


def square_off_all(portfolio: PortfolioManager, engine: ExecutionEngine, latest_ticks: dict, replay_mode: bool = False):
    open_positions = list(portfolio.positions.keys())
    if not open_positions:
        return

    print(f"\n[SYSTEM] Initiating forced 3:14 PM liquidation of {len(open_positions)} positions.")
    push_alert(1, "ENGINE", f"🚨 3:14 PM Square-Off: Closing {len(open_positions)} open positions.")

    for open_sec_id in open_positions:
        try:
            pos_data = portfolio.positions.get(open_sec_id)
            if not pos_data:
                continue

            pos_qty = pos_data['qty']
            side = pos_data['side']
            entry_p = pos_data['avg_price']
            sym = pos_data.get('symbol', f"TOK_{open_sec_id}")
            exit_action = 'SELL' if side == 'BUY' else 'BUY'
            trace_id = f"{open_sec_id}-SQUAREOFF-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"

            cached_tick = latest_ticks.get(open_sec_id)
            if cached_tick:
                market_ltp = cached_tick.get('ltp', 0.0)
                market_bid = cached_tick.get('bid', market_ltp)
                market_ask = cached_tick.get('ask', market_ltp)
                ts_val = cached_tick.get('timestamp', datetime.now().isoformat())
            else:
                market_ltp = entry_p or 1.0
                market_bid = market_ltp
                market_ask = market_ltp
                ts_val = datetime.now().isoformat()

            exit_tick = {
                'security_id': open_sec_id,
                'ltp': market_ltp,
                'price': market_ltp,
                'ask': market_ask,
                'bid': market_bid,
                'best_ask_vol': pos_qty,
                'best_bid_vol': pos_qty,
                'timestamp': ts_val
            }

            engine.execute_paper_trade(
                exit_tick, action=exit_action, qty=pos_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                bypass_margin=True, symbol=sym, trigger_source="EOD_CUTOFF", trigger_reason="3:14_PM_SQUAREOFF"
            )
            est_pnl = ((market_ltp - entry_p) * pos_qty) if side == 'BUY' else ((entry_p - market_ltp) * pos_qty)
            push_alert(
                2, "EXECUTION",
                f"🛑 <b>3:14 PM SQUARE-OFF ({exit_action})</b>: <code>{sym}</code>\n"
                f"Qty: {pos_qty} | Entry: ₹{entry_p:.2f} -> Exit: ₹{market_ltp:.2f} | Gross: ₹{est_pnl:+.2f}"
            )
            if open_sec_id in portfolio.positions:
                del portfolio.positions[open_sec_id]
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"  [ERROR] Failed squaring off {open_sec_id}: {e}")


def main():
    r = redis.Redis(
        host=os.getenv("REDIS_HOST", "localhost"),
        port=int(os.getenv("REDIS_PORT", 6379)),
        decode_responses=True
    )

    max_slots = int(os.getenv("MAX_CONCURRENT_POSITIONS", 4))
    portfolio = PortfolioManager(
        starting_balance=100000.0,
        max_allocation_pct=0.20,
        max_loss_pct=0.02,
        max_concurrent_positions=max_slots
    )
    logger = AuditLogger()
    engine = ExecutionEngine(portfolio, logger)
    signal_logger = setup_signal_logger()
    ist_tz = pytz.timezone('Asia/Kolkata')

    r.delete("EMERGENCY_STOP")
    sync_live_redis_state(r, portfolio)

    # Exact Live Indicators
    spread = BidAskSpread()
    obi = OrderBookImbalance(alpha=0.1)
    cvd = CumulativeVolumeDelta(window=500)
    wmp = WeightedMidPrice()
    atr = TickATR(period=14, tick_chunk=50)
    vwap = TickVWAP()
    indicators = [spread, obi, cvd, wmp, atr, vwap]

    last_trade_time = {}
    COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 30.0
    signal_streak = {}
    latest_ticks = {}

    breakeven_locked = set()
    partial_tp_executed = set()
    pyramid_executed = set()
    velocity_buffers = {}
    micro_bots = {}
    autopsy_records = []

    replay_mode = r.get("replay_mode")
    if replay_mode == "stream":
        tick_count = r.xlen("replay_stream")
        print(f"[MODE] REPLAY via Redis Stream | Max Concurrent Slots: {max_slots} | Total Stream Ticks: {tick_count:,}")
        send_tg_event("REPLAY ENGINE", "START", f"Replaying {tick_count:,} ticks with restored Live Winning Math ({max_slots} slots).")
        last_stream_id = "0-0"
        pubsub = None
    else:
        replay_mode = None
        tick_count = 0
        print(f"[MODE] LIVE via Redis Pub/Sub | Max Concurrent Slots: {max_slots}")
        pubsub = r.pubsub()
        pubsub.subscribe("live_ticks")
        push_alert(1, "ENGINE", f"🚀 Micro-Bot Engine Online (LIVE) | Max Concurrent Slots: {max_slots}")
        send_tg_event("LIVE ENGINE", "START", f"Micro-Bot Engine Online | Max Concurrent Slots: {max_slots}")

    cutoff_time = time(15, 14, 0)
    no_new_entries_time = time(15, 0, 0)
    processed = 0
    equity_ticks_processed = 0
    cutoff_confirm_streak = 0
    stream_batch = deque()
    last_heartbeat_min = -1

    while True:
        if not replay_mode:
            now_ist = datetime.now(ist_tz)
            current_time = now_ist.time()
            if now_ist.minute % 15 == 0 and now_ist.minute != last_heartbeat_min:
                last_heartbeat_min = now_ist.minute
                send_tg_event(
                    "LIVE HEARTBEAT", "SUCCESS",
                    f"Ticks: {processed:,} | Active Slots: {len(portfolio.positions)}/{max_slots} | Balance: ₹{portfolio.current_balance:,.2f}"
                )
            if current_time >= cutoff_time:
                print("\n[CUTOFF] 3:14 PM reached (wall clock).")
                square_off_all(portfolio, engine, latest_ticks, replay_mode=False)
                sync_live_redis_state(r, portfolio)
                run_end_of_day_reporting(portfolio, autopsy_records, replay_mode=False)
                break

        tick = None
        if replay_mode:
            if not stream_batch:
                results = r.xread({"replay_stream": last_stream_id}, count=2500, block=500)
                if not results:
                    print(f"\n[REPLAY COMPLETE] Processed {processed:,} total stream ticks ({equity_ticks_processed:,} equity ticks).")
                    square_off_all(portfolio, engine, latest_ticks, replay_mode=True)
                    sync_live_redis_state(r, portfolio, processed=processed, active_bots=len(micro_bots), mode="REPLAY_DONE")
                    run_end_of_day_reporting(portfolio, autopsy_records, replay_mode=True)
                    r.delete("replay_mode")
                    r.delete("replay_stream")
                    break
                stream_batch.extend(results[0][1])

            entry_id, entry_data = stream_batch.popleft()
            last_stream_id = entry_id
            tick = json.loads(entry_data["data"])
            tick_epoch_ms = int(tick.get('timestamp', 0))
            current_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(tick_epoch_ms / 1000.0, tz=ist_tz).time()

            processed += 1
            if processed % 100000 == 0:
                pct = (processed / (tick_count or 1)) * 100
                print(
                    f"  [REPLAY] {processed:>7,} / {tick_count:,} ticks ({pct:5.1f}%) | "
                    f"Time: {str(current_time)[:8]} | Slots: {len(portfolio.positions)}/{max_slots} | "
                    f"Balance: Rs.{portfolio.current_balance:,.2f}"
                )

            if current_time >= cutoff_time:
                cutoff_confirm_streak += 1
                if cutoff_confirm_streak >= 5 and len(portfolio.positions) > 0:
                    square_off_all(portfolio, engine, latest_ticks, replay_mode=True)
                continue
            else:
                cutoff_confirm_streak = 0
        else:
            message = pubsub.get_message(ignore_subscribe_messages=True, timeout=1.0)
            if not (message and message['type'] == 'message'):
                continue
            tick = json.loads(message['data'])
            tick_epoch_ms = int(tick.get('timestamp', 0))
            current_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(tick_epoch_ms / 1000.0, tz=ist_tz).time()
            processed += 1

        if not tick:
            continue

        raw_sec_id = tick.get('security_id')
        if not raw_sec_id:
            continue
        sec_id = str(raw_sec_id)

        if sec_id in EXCLUDED_TOKENS:
            continue

        equity_ticks_processed += 1
        if equity_ticks_processed % 500 == 0:
            sync_live_redis_state(
                r, portfolio, str(current_time)[:8],
                processed, len(micro_bots), "REPLAY" if replay_mode else "LIVE"
            )
            if r.get("EMERGENCY_STOP") == "1":
                print("\n[FATAL] Emergency Stop flag detected in Redis. Halting operations.")
                square_off_all(portfolio, engine, latest_ticks, replay_mode=bool(replay_mode))
                run_end_of_day_reporting(portfolio, autopsy_records, replay_mode=bool(replay_mode))
                r.delete("EMERGENCY_STOP")
                break

        tick_ltp = float(tick.get('ltp', 0.0))
        if tick_ltp <= 0.0:
            continue
        latest_ticks[sec_id] = tick

        for ind in indicators:
            ind.update(tick)

        obi_score = obi.get_score(sec_id)
        cvd_score = cvd.get_score(sec_id)
        current_atr = atr.get_score(sec_id)
        current_vwap = vwap.get_score(sec_id)

        bot = micro_bots.get(sec_id)
        if bot is None:
            bot = StockMicroBot(sec_id)
            micro_bots[sec_id] = bot
        bot.on_tick(tick_epoch_ms, tick_ltp, int(tick.get('ltq', 1)), current_vwap, current_atr)
        sym = bot.symbol

        # =================================================================
        # 5. EXACT LIVE POSITION MANAGEMENT (SL=1.5%, TP=3.0%, BE=1.0%, PARTIAL=1.5%, PYRAMID)
        # =================================================================
        current_pos = portfolio.positions.get(sec_id)
        if current_pos:
            entry_price = current_pos['avg_price']
            pos_qty = current_pos['qty']
            side = current_pos['side']

            SL_PERCENT = 0.015
            TP_PERCENT = 0.030
            BREAKEVEN_TRIGGER = 0.010
            PARTIAL_TP_TRIGGER = 0.015
            TAX_OFFSET_PCT = 0.002

            if side == 'BUY':
                tp_price = entry_price * (1.0 + TP_PERCENT)
                base_sl_price = entry_price * (1.0 - SL_PERCENT)

                if tick_ltp >= entry_price * (1.0 + BREAKEVEN_TRIGGER):
                    breakeven_locked.add(sec_id)

                if sec_id not in partial_tp_executed and tick_ltp >= entry_price * (1.0 + PARTIAL_TP_TRIGGER):
                    scale_qty = max(1, int(pos_qty * 0.5))
                    trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
                    engine.execute_paper_trade(
                        tick, action='SELL', qty=scale_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                        bypass_margin=True, symbol=sym, trigger_source="POSITION_MGR", trigger_reason="PARTIAL_TP_+1.50%"
                    )
                    partial_tp_executed.add(sec_id)
                    gross_leg = (tick_ltp - entry_price) * scale_qty
                    push_alert(
                        2, "EXECUTION",
                        f"⚡ <b>PARTIAL TP (Long)</b>: <code>{sym}</code> | Scaled out {scale_qty}x @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f} | Gross: ₹{gross_leg:+.2f}"
                    )
                    continue

                if sec_id in breakeven_locked:
                    if sec_id in pyramid_executed:
                        sl_price = entry_price * 0.995
                    else:
                        sl_price = entry_price * (1.0 + TAX_OFFSET_PCT)
                else:
                    sl_price = base_sl_price

                if tick_ltp >= tp_price or tick_ltp <= sl_price:
                    trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
                    reason = "FINAL_TAKE_PROFIT" if tick_ltp >= tp_price else ("BREAKEVEN_SL" if sec_id in breakeven_locked else "STOP_LOSS")
                    engine.execute_paper_trade(
                        tick, action='SELL', qty=pos_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                        bypass_margin=True, symbol=sym, trigger_source="POSITION_MGR", trigger_reason=reason
                    )
                    if sec_id in portfolio.positions:
                        del portfolio.positions[sec_id]
                    breakeven_locked.discard(sec_id)
                    partial_tp_executed.discard(sec_id)
                    pyramid_executed.discard(sec_id)
                    gross_leg = (tick_ltp - entry_price) * pos_qty
                    push_alert(
                        2, "EXECUTION",
                        f"🛑 <b>{reason} (Long)</b>: <code>{sym}</code> | Closed {pos_qty}x @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f} | Gross: ₹{gross_leg:+.2f}"
                    )
                    continue

                if sec_id in breakeven_locked and sec_id not in pyramid_executed:
                    if cvd_score > 1500:
                        add_qty = max(1, int(pos_qty * 0.5))
                        trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
                        status = engine.execute_paper_trade(
                            tick, action='BUY', qty=add_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                            symbol=sym, trigger_source="PYRAMID", trigger_reason="PYRAMID_CVD_+1500"
                        )
                        pyramid_executed.add(sec_id)
                        if status == "EXECUTED":
                            push_alert(2, "EXECUTION", f"🔼 <b>PYRAMID (Long)</b>: Added {add_qty}x <code>{sym}</code> @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f}")
                        continue

            elif side == 'SELL':
                tp_price = entry_price * (1.0 - TP_PERCENT)
                base_sl_price = entry_price * (1.0 + SL_PERCENT)

                if tick_ltp <= entry_price * (1.0 - BREAKEVEN_TRIGGER):
                    breakeven_locked.add(sec_id)

                if sec_id not in partial_tp_executed and tick_ltp <= entry_price * (1.0 - PARTIAL_TP_TRIGGER):
                    scale_qty = max(1, int(pos_qty * 0.5))
                    trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
                    engine.execute_paper_trade(
                        tick, action='BUY', qty=scale_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                        bypass_margin=True, symbol=sym, trigger_source="POSITION_MGR", trigger_reason="PARTIAL_TP_-1.50%"
                    )
                    partial_tp_executed.add(sec_id)
                    gross_leg = (entry_price - tick_ltp) * scale_qty
                    push_alert(
                        2, "EXECUTION",
                        f"⚡ <b>PARTIAL TP (Short)</b>: <code>{sym}</code> | Scaled out {scale_qty}x @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f} | Gross: ₹{gross_leg:+.2f}"
                    )
                    continue

                if sec_id in breakeven_locked:
                    if sec_id in pyramid_executed:
                        sl_price = entry_price * 1.005
                    else:
                        sl_price = entry_price * (1.0 - TAX_OFFSET_PCT)
                else:
                    sl_price = base_sl_price

                if tick_ltp <= tp_price or tick_ltp >= sl_price:
                    trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
                    reason = "FINAL_TAKE_PROFIT" if tick_ltp <= tp_price else ("BREAKEVEN_SL" if sec_id in breakeven_locked else "STOP_LOSS")
                    engine.execute_paper_trade(
                        tick, action='BUY', qty=pos_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                        bypass_margin=True, symbol=sym, trigger_source="POSITION_MGR", trigger_reason=reason
                    )
                    if sec_id in portfolio.positions:
                        del portfolio.positions[sec_id]
                    breakeven_locked.discard(sec_id)
                    partial_tp_executed.discard(sec_id)
                    pyramid_executed.discard(sec_id)
                    gross_leg = (entry_price - tick_ltp) * pos_qty
                    push_alert(
                        2, "EXECUTION",
                        f"🛑 <b>{reason} (Short)</b>: <code>{sym}</code> | Closed {pos_qty}x @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f} | Gross: ₹{gross_leg:+.2f}"
                    )
                    continue

                if sec_id in breakeven_locked and sec_id not in pyramid_executed:
                    if cvd_score < -1500:
                        add_qty = max(1, int(pos_qty * 0.5))
                        trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
                        status = engine.execute_paper_trade(
                            tick, action='SELL', qty=add_qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                            symbol=sym, trigger_source="PYRAMID", trigger_reason="PYRAMID_CVD_-1500"
                        )
                        pyramid_executed.add(sec_id)
                        if status == "EXECUTED":
                            push_alert(2, "EXECUTION", f"🔼 <b>PYRAMID (Short)</b>: Added {add_qty}x <code>{sym}</code> @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f}")
                        continue
        else:
            breakeven_locked.discard(sec_id)
            partial_tp_executed.discard(sec_id)
            pyramid_executed.discard(sec_id)

        # =================================================================
        # 6. EXACT LIVE ENTRY STRATEGY LOGIC + MICRO-BOT AUTHORITY
        # =================================================================
        if current_atr == 0.0:
            continue

        tick_bid = float(tick.get('bid', tick_ltp))
        tick_ask = float(tick.get('ask', tick_ltp))
        natural_spread = tick_ask - tick_bid

        if natural_spread > (0.08 * current_atr):
            continue

        if current_time >= no_new_entries_time:
            continue

        MIN_POSITION_VALUE = 5000.0
        tick_ts = tick_epoch_ms / 1000.0

        v_buffer = velocity_buffers.get(sec_id)
        if v_buffer is None:
            v_buffer = deque()
            velocity_buffers[sec_id] = v_buffer

        current_ts_ms = tick_ts * 1000.0
        v_buffer.append((current_ts_ms, obi_score, cvd_score))

        while len(v_buffer) > 1 and (current_ts_ms - v_buffer[0][0]) > 6000.0:
            v_buffer.popleft()

        if not v_buffer or (current_ts_ms - v_buffer[0][0]) < 1500.0:
            continue

        old_ts, old_obi, old_cvd = v_buffer[0]
        delta_obi = obi_score - old_obi
        delta_cvd = cvd_score - old_cvd

        vwap_buffer = current_vwap * 0.002
        upper_vwap_band = current_vwap + vwap_buffer
        lower_vwap_band = current_vwap - vwap_buffer

        OBI_THRESHOLD = 0.20
        CVD_THRESHOLD = max(50.0, 100000.0 / max(tick_ltp, 1.0))

        is_bearish = (tick_ltp > lower_vwap_band) and (delta_obi < -OBI_THRESHOLD) and (delta_cvd < -CVD_THRESHOLD)
        is_bullish = (tick_ltp < upper_vwap_band) and (delta_obi > OBI_THRESHOLD) and (delta_cvd > CVD_THRESHOLD)

        if is_bearish:
            current_signal = "SELL"
        elif is_bullish:
            current_signal = "BUY"
        else:
            signal_streak[sec_id] = (None, 0)
            continue

        prev_signal, prev_count = signal_streak.get(sec_id, (None, 0))
        if prev_signal == current_signal:
            signal_streak[sec_id] = (current_signal, prev_count + 1)
        else:
            signal_streak[sec_id] = (current_signal, 1)

        streak_count = signal_streak[sec_id][1]
        if streak_count < 3:
            continue

        if current_pos:
            continue

        if tick_ts - last_trade_time.get(sec_id, 0.0) < COOLDOWN_SECONDS:
            continue

        approved, trigger_source, reg_label = bot.evaluate_signal_authority(current_signal)
        if not approved:
            continue

        # Exact Live Kelly Scaling & Order-Book Liquidity Cap
        cvd_edge = min(1.0, max(0.0, (abs(delta_cvd) - CVD_THRESHOLD) / 600.0))
        obi_edge = min(1.0, max(0.0, (abs(delta_obi) - OBI_THRESHOLD) / 0.25))
        spread_edge = max(0.0, 1.0 - (natural_spread / (0.08 * current_atr)))

        confluence_score = (cvd_edge + obi_edge + spread_edge) / 3.0
        calculated_target = 10000.0 + (confluence_score * 15000.0)
        target_capital = min(calculated_target, 20000.0)

        free_cash = portfolio.get_idle_capital()
        if free_cash < (MIN_POSITION_VALUE / 5.0):
            continue

        ideal_qty = int(target_capital / tick_ltp)
        available_vol = int(tick.get('best_bid_vol', 0) if current_signal == "SELL" else tick.get('best_ask_vol', 0)) or ideal_qty
        qty = min(ideal_qty, available_vol, int((free_cash * 5.0) / tick_ltp))

        if qty > 0 and (qty * tick_ltp) >= 4900.0:
            trace_id = f"{sec_id}-{tick_epoch_ms}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
            trigger_reason = (
                f"{trigger_source}[{reg_label}] | dCVD:{delta_cvd:+.0f}(Thr:{CVD_THRESHOLD:.0f}) "
                f"| dOBI:{delta_obi:+.2f} | LTP:{tick_ltp:.2f} vs VWAP:{current_vwap:.2f}"
            )
            status = engine.execute_paper_trade(
                tick, action=current_signal, qty=qty, trace_id=trace_id,
                symbol=sym, trigger_source=trigger_source, trigger_reason=trigger_reason
            )

            if status == "EXECUTED":
                last_trade_time[sec_id] = tick_ts
                push_alert(
                    2,
                    "EXECUTION",
                    f"🟢 <b>ENTRY ({current_signal})</b>: <code>{sym}</code> (#{sec_id})\n"
                    f"Qty: <b>{qty}</b> @ ₹{tick_ltp:.2f} (Notional: ₹{qty*tick_ltp:,.0f})\n"
                    f"Brain: <b>{trigger_source}</b> | Conf: <b>{confluence_score:.2f}</b>"
                )

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

from .base import BaseIndicator

class TickATR(BaseIndicator):
    def __init__(self, period: int = 14, tick_chunk: int = 50):
        super().__init__("TickATR")
        self.period = period
        self.tick_chunk = tick_chunk  # How many ticks make up 1 "candle"
        
        # Isolated tracking state per security
        self.ticks_seen = {}
        self.current_high = {}
        self.current_low = {}
        self.prev_close = {}
        self.tr_history = {}

    def update(self, tick: dict):
        sec_id = str(tick.get('security_id'))
        self._initialize_state(sec_id)  # From BaseIndicator

        # Initialize ATR-specific memory blocks for new securities
        if sec_id not in self.ticks_seen:
            self.ticks_seen[sec_id] = 0
            self.current_high[sec_id] = -float('inf')
            self.current_low[sec_id] = float('inf')
            self.prev_close[sec_id] = None
            self.tr_history[sec_id] = []

        ltp = tick.get('ltp', 0.0)
        if ltp == 0.0:
            return

        # Initialize bounds on first tick
        if self.current_high[sec_id] == -float('inf'):
            self.current_high[sec_id] = ltp
            self.current_low[sec_id] = ltp

        # Track High/Low dynamically for the current chunk
        self.current_high[sec_id] = max(self.current_high[sec_id], ltp)
        self.current_low[sec_id] = min(self.current_low[sec_id], ltp)
        self.ticks_seen[sec_id] += 1

        # Once chunk is full, calculate True Range and average it
        if self.ticks_seen[sec_id] >= self.tick_chunk:
            if self.prev_close[sec_id] is None:
                tr = self.current_high[sec_id] - self.current_low[sec_id]
            else:
                tr = max(
                    self.current_high[sec_id] - self.current_low[sec_id],
                    abs(self.current_high[sec_id] - self.prev_close[sec_id]),
                    abs(self.current_low[sec_id] - self.prev_close[sec_id])
                )
            
            self.tr_history[sec_id].append(tr)
            if len(self.tr_history[sec_id]) > self.period:
                self.tr_history[sec_id].pop(0)

            # Simple Moving Average of True Range = ATR
            self.values[sec_id] = sum(self.tr_history[sec_id]) / len(self.tr_history[sec_id])

            # Reset state for the next tick chunk
            self.prev_close[sec_id] = ltp
            self.current_high[sec_id] = ltp
            self.current_low[sec_id] = ltp
            self.ticks_seen[sec_id] = 0

FILE: .\src\trading\indicators\base.py
============================================================
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

class BaseIndicator(ABC):
    def __init__(self, name: str):
        self.name = name
        # Memory is now strictly isolated by security ID
        self.values = {}
        self.history = {}

    def _initialize_state(self, sec_id: str):
        """Allocates an isolated memory block for a new stock on its first tick."""
        if sec_id not in self.values:
            self.values[sec_id] = 0.0
            self.history[sec_id] = []

    @abstractmethod
    def update(self, tick: dict):
        """
        Process new tick data.
        Child classes MUST call sec_id = str(tick['security_id']) 
        and run self._initialize_state(sec_id) before processing math.
        """
        pass

    def get_score(self, sec_id: str) -> float:
        """
        Returns the current calculated score for a specific security.
        The signature now requires a strict memory address lookup.
        """
        # Force string conversion to prevent dictionary key mismatches
        sec_id = str(sec_id)
        return self.values.get(sec_id, 0.0)

FILE: .\src\trading\indicators\cvd.py

from collections import deque
from .base import BaseIndicator

class CumulativeVolumeDelta(BaseIndicator):
    def __init__(self, window: int = 500):
        super().__init__("CVD")
        self.window = window
        self._deltas = {}
        self._prev_ltp = {}

    def update(self, tick: dict):
        sec_id = str(tick.get('security_id'))
        self._initialize_state(sec_id)

        if sec_id not in self._deltas:
            self._deltas[sec_id] = deque(maxlen=self.window)
            self._prev_ltp[sec_id] = 0.0

        ltp = float(tick.get('ltp', 0.0))
        ltq = int(tick.get('ltq', 0))
        bid = float(tick.get('bid', 0.0))
        ask = float(tick.get('ask', 0.0))

        if not sec_id or ltp <= 0.0 or ltq <= 0:
            return

        mid = (bid + ask) / 2.0 if (bid > 0 and ask > 0) else ltp
        prev_p = self._prev_ltp[sec_id]
        self._prev_ltp[sec_id] = ltp

        if ltp > mid:
            delta = ltq
        elif ltp < mid:
            delta = -ltq
        elif prev_p > 0 and ltp != prev_p:
            delta = ltq if ltp > prev_p else -ltq
        else:
            delta = 0

        self._deltas[sec_id].append(delta)
        self.values[sec_id] = sum(self._deltas[sec_id])

FILE: .\src\trading\indicators\obi.py

from .base import BaseIndicator

class OrderBookImbalance(BaseIndicator):
    def __init__(self, alpha: float = 0.1):
        super().__init__("OBI")
        self.alpha = alpha
        self._raw_obi = {}

    def update(self, tick: dict):
        sec_id = str(tick.get('security_id'))
        self._initialize_state(sec_id)

        bid_vol = tick.get('best_bid_vol', 0)
        ask_vol = tick.get('best_ask_vol', 0)

        total_vol = bid_vol + ask_vol

        if total_vol == 0:
            raw_obi = 0.0
        else:
            raw_obi = (bid_vol - ask_vol) / total_vol

        if sec_id not in self._raw_obi:
            self._raw_obi[sec_id] = raw_obi
        else:
            self._raw_obi[sec_id] = (self.alpha * raw_obi) + ((1.0 - self.alpha) * self._raw_obi[sec_id])

        self.values[sec_id] = self._raw_obi[sec_id]

FILE: .\src\trading\indicators\spread.py

from .base import BaseIndicator

class BidAskSpread(BaseIndicator):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__("Normalized_Spread")

    def update(self, tick: dict):
        sec_id = str(tick.get('security_id'))
        self._initialize_state(sec_id)

        bid = tick.get('bid', 0.0)
        ask = tick.get('ask', 0.0)
        
        # Protect against dead ticks and division by zero
        if ask == 0.0 or bid == 0.0:
            self.values[sec_id] = 0.0
        else:
            # Normalized spread ratio
            self.values[sec_id] = (ask - bid) / ask

FILE: .\src\trading\indicators\vwap.py

from .base import BaseIndicator

class TickVWAP(BaseIndicator):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__("TickVWAP")
        # Isolate memory for cumulative Price*Volume and Volume per security
        self.cumulative_pv = {}
        self.cumulative_vol = {}

    def update(self, tick: dict):
        sec_id = str(tick.get('security_id'))
        self._initialize_state(sec_id)

        if sec_id not in self.cumulative_pv:
            self.cumulative_pv[sec_id] = 0.0
            self.cumulative_vol[sec_id] = 0

        ltp = float(tick.get('ltp', 0.0))
        # Handle both live (last_traded_quantity) and replay (ltq) tick formats
        raw_vol = tick.get('last_traded_quantity') or tick.get('ltq') or tick.get('v')
        vol = float(raw_vol) if raw_vol is not None and float(raw_vol) > 0 else 0.0

        if ltp == 0.0 or vol <= 0.0:
            return

        # VWAP Math: Sum(Price * Volume) / Sum(Volume)
        self.cumulative_pv[sec_id] += (ltp * vol)
        self.cumulative_vol[sec_id] += vol

        if self.cumulative_vol[sec_id] > 0:
            self.values[sec_id] = self.cumulative_pv[sec_id] / self.cumulative_vol[sec_id]
        else:
            self.values[sec_id] = ltp

from .base import BaseIndicator

class WeightedMidPrice(BaseIndicator):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__("WMP")

    def update(self, tick: dict):
        sec_id = str(tick.get('security_id'))
        self._initialize_state(sec_id)

        bid = tick.get('bid', 0.0)
        ask = tick.get('ask', 0.0)
        bid_vol = tick.get('best_bid_vol', 0)
        ask_vol = tick.get('best_ask_vol', 0)

        total_vol = bid_vol + ask_vol

        # Fallback to standard mid if volume data drops to prevent zero-division
        if total_vol == 0 or bid == 0.0 or ask == 0.0:
            self.values[sec_id] = (bid + ask) / 2.0 if (bid and ask) else 0.0
        else:
            # Cross-weighted math
            self.values[sec_id] = (bid * ask_vol + ask * bid_vol) / total_vol

FILE: .\src\utils\alert_publisher.py
============================================================
import os
import json
import time
import threading
import requests
import redis
from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()

r = redis.Redis(
    host=os.getenv("REDIS_HOST", "localhost"),
    port=int(os.getenv("REDIS_PORT", 6379)),
    decode_responses=True
)

BOT_TOKEN = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN", "").strip()
DEFAULT_CHAT_ID = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID", "").strip()
TIERS = {
    1: os.getenv("TIER1_CHAT_ID") or DEFAULT_CHAT_ID,
    2: os.getenv("TIER2_CHAT_ID") or DEFAULT_CHAT_ID,
    3: os.getenv("TIER3_CHAT_ID") or DEFAULT_CHAT_ID,
}

def _direct_tg_send(tier: int, source: str, message: str):
    if not BOT_TOKEN:
        return
    chat_id = TIERS.get(int(tier)) or DEFAULT_CHAT_ID
    if not chat_id:
        return
    icons = {1: "🚨", 2: "⚡", 3: "⚙️"}
    text = f"{icons.get(int(tier), '🔔')} <b>[{source}]</b>\n{message}"
    url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{BOT_TOKEN}/sendMessage"
    try:
        requests.post(
            url,
            json={"chat_id": chat_id, "text": text, "parse_mode": "HTML", "disable_web_page_preview": True},
            timeout=6
        )
    except Exception:
        pass

def push_alert(tier: int, source: str, message: str, force_direct: bool = False):
    """
    Routes alerts to Telegram.
    If telegram_listener.py is not draining Redis (e.g. local Windows replay),
    automatically dispatches via background HTTP thread so no execution log is ever missed.
    """
    payload = {
        "tier": tier,
        "source": source,
        "message": message,
        "timestamp": int(time.time() * 1000)
    }
    try:
        q_len = r.rpush("sys_alerts_queue", json.dumps(payload))
        # If queue is backing up (>2 items), listener daemon isn't running -> send directly
        if force_direct or q_len > 2:
            r.ltrim("sys_alerts_queue", -1, 0)
            threading.Thread(target=_direct_tg_send, args=(tier, source, message), daemon=True).start()
    except Exception:
        threading.Thread(target=_direct_tg_send, args=(tier, source, message), daemon=True).start()

FILE: .\src\utils\api_tracker.py

import os
import json
import time
import asyncio
import requests
from datetime import datetime
from dotenv import load_dotenv

PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "../../"))
load_dotenv(os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, ".env"))

QUOTA_FILE = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "data", "api_quota_ledger.json")
os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(QUOTA_FILE), exist_ok=True)

MONTHLY_LIMITS = {
    "ANGEL_ONE": int(os.getenv("ANGEL_MONTHLY_LIMIT", 150000)),
    "DHAN": int(os.getenv("DHAN_MONTHLY_LIMIT", 250000)),
    "GEMINI": int(os.getenv("GEMINI_MONTHLY_LIMIT", 45000)),
    "GROQ": int(os.getenv("GROQ_MONTHLY_LIMIT", 45000)),
}

DAILY_LIMITS = {
    "ANGEL_ONE": int(os.getenv("ANGEL_DAILY_LIMIT", 8000)),
    "DHAN": int(os.getenv("DHAN_DAILY_LIMIT", 15000)),
    "GEMINI": int(os.getenv("GEMINI_DAILY_LIMIT", 1500)),
    "GROQ": int(os.getenv("GROQ_DAILY_LIMIT", 14400)),
}

_CACHED_DHAN_TOKEN = None


def get_dhan_token() -> str:
    """
    Resolves Dhan token using DHAN_CLIENT_ID, DHAN_PIN, DHAN_TOTP_SECRET, DHAN_API_KEY.
    Attempts TOTP session via DhanAuthenticator first, falling back to DHAN_API_KEY.
    """
    global _CACHED_DHAN_TOKEN
    if _CACHED_DHAN_TOKEN:
        return _CACHED_DHAN_TOKEN

    client_id = os.getenv("DHAN_CLIENT_ID")
    pin = os.getenv("DHAN_PIN")
    totp_secret = os.getenv("DHAN_TOTP_SECRET")
    api_key = os.getenv("DHAN_API_KEY")

    if client_id and pin and totp_secret:
        try:
            from src.auth.dhan_auth import DhanAuthenticator
            auth = DhanAuthenticator()
            token = asyncio.run(auth.get_session(max_retries=1))
            if token and isinstance(token, str):
                _CACHED_DHAN_TOKEN = token
                return _CACHED_DHAN_TOKEN
        except Exception:
            pass

    if api_key:
        _CACHED_DHAN_TOKEN = api_key
        return _CACHED_DHAN_TOKEN
    return ""


class APITracker:
    @staticmethod
    def _load() -> dict:
        today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        month = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m")
        default = {"day": today, "month": month, "providers": {}}
        if os.path.exists(QUOTA_FILE):
            try:
                with open(QUOTA_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                if data.get("month") != month:
                    data = {"day": today, "month": month, "providers": {}}
                elif data.get("day") != today:
                    data["day"] = today
                    for p in data.get("providers", {}).values():
                        p["daily_calls"] = 0
                        p["daily_errors"] = 0
                return data
            except Exception:
                pass
        return default

    @staticmethod
    def _save(data: dict):
        try:
            with open(QUOTA_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=4)
        except Exception:
            pass

    @classmethod
    def record(cls, provider: str, success: bool = True, rate_limited: bool = False):
        data = cls._load()
        p = data["providers"].setdefault(provider, {
            "daily_calls": 0,
            "monthly_calls": 0,
            "daily_errors": 0,
            "rate_limit_hits": 0,
            "last_call": ""
        })
        p["daily_calls"] += 1
        p["monthly_calls"] += 1
        if not success:
            p["daily_errors"] += 1
        if rate_limited:
            p["rate_limit_hits"] += 1
        p["last_call"] = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        cls._save(data)

    @classmethod
    def is_angel_exhausted(cls) -> bool:
        data = cls._load()
        p = data.get("providers", {}).get("ANGEL_ONE", {})
        if p.get("daily_calls", 0) >= DAILY_LIMITS["ANGEL_ONE"]:
            return True
        if p.get("monthly_calls", 0) >= MONTHLY_LIMITS["ANGEL_ONE"]:
            return True
        return False

    @classmethod
    def get_summary_text(cls) -> str:
        data = cls._load()
        lines = [f"📊 <b>API Quota Ledger ({data['day']})</b>"]
        for prov in ["ANGEL_ONE", "DHAN", "GEMINI", "GROQ"]:
            p = data.get("providers", {}).get(prov, {})
            d_used = p.get("daily_calls", 0)
            m_used = p.get("monthly_calls", 0)
            rl = p.get("rate_limit_hits", 0)
            d_lim = DAILY_LIMITS.get(prov, 10000)
            m_lim = MONTHLY_LIMITS.get(prov, 100000)
            lines.append(
                f"• <b>{prov}</b>: Day <code>{d_used}/{d_lim}</code> | "
                f"Month <code>{m_used}/{m_lim}</code> | 429s: <code>{rl}</code>"
            )
        return "\n".join(lines)


def fetch_dhan_historical(token: str, interval: str, from_date: str, to_date: str) -> list:
    """
    Fallback fetcher using Dhan v2 Historical / Intraday Charts API.
    Uses DHAN_CLIENT_ID, DHAN_PIN, DHAN_TOTP_SECRET, DHAN_API_KEY from .env.
    """
    access_token = get_dhan_token()
    client_id = os.getenv("DHAN_CLIENT_ID", "")
    if not access_token:
        return []

    headers = {
        "access-token": access_token,
        "client-id": client_id,
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "Accept": "application/json"
    }
    is_intraday = interval != "ONE_DAY"
    url = "https://api.dhan.co/v2/charts/intraday" if is_intraday else "https://api.dhan.co/v2/charts/historical"
    payload = {
        "securityId": str(token),
        "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
        "instrument": "EQUITY",
        "fromDate": from_date[:10],
        "toDate": to_date[:10]
    }
    if is_intraday:
        payload["interval"] = "1"

    try:
        resp = requests.post(url, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=10)
        APITracker.record("DHAN", success=(resp.status_code == 200), rate_limited=(resp.status_code == 429))
        if resp.status_code == 200:
            d = resp.json()
            opens = d.get("open", [])
            highs = d.get("high", [])
            lows = d.get("low", [])
            closes = d.get("close", [])
            vols = d.get("volume", [])
            ts_list = d.get("timestamp", [])
            rows = []
            for i in range(min(len(opens), len(ts_list))):
                dt_iso = datetime.fromtimestamp(int(ts_list[i])).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S")
                rows.append([dt_iso, float(opens[i]), float(highs[i]), float(lows[i]), float(closes[i]), int(vols[i])])
            return rows
    except Exception:
        APITracker.record("DHAN", success=False)
    return []


def fetch_candles_with_fallback(angel_client, token: str, interval: str, from_str: str, to_str: str) -> list:
    """
    Primary: Angel One SmartAPI.
    Automatic Failover: Switches to Dhan API if Angel One quota is exhausted, throws b'', or hits rate limits.
    """
    if not APITracker.is_angel_exhausted() and angel_client is not None:
        try:
            params = {
                "exchange": "NSE",
                "symboltoken": str(token),
                "interval": interval,
                "fromdate": from_str,
                "todate": to_str
            }
            resp = angel_client.getCandleData(params)
            time.sleep(0.38)
            if isinstance(resp, dict) and resp.get("status") and resp.get("data"):
                APITracker.record("ANGEL_ONE", success=True)
                return resp["data"]
            msg = str(resp.get("message", "") if isinstance(resp, dict) else "").lower()
            is_rl = ("limit" in msg) or ("throttle" in msg) or ("exceed" in msg)
            APITracker.record("ANGEL_ONE", success=False, rate_limited=is_rl)
        except Exception:
            APITracker.record("ANGEL_ONE", success=False, rate_limited=True)

    return fetch_dhan_historical(str(token), interval, from_str, to_str)

FILE: .\src\utils\fault_guard.py
============================================================
import sys
import traceback
import functools
from src.utils.alert_publisher import push_alert
from src.utils.tg_notifier import send_tg_event

def report_fault(source: str, exc: BaseException):
    tb_short = "".join(traceback.format_exception_only(type(exc), exc)).strip()
    msg = f"⚠️ <b>FAULT [{source}]</b>: <code>{tb_short[:220]}</code>"
    try:
        push_alert(1, source, msg)
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        send_tg_event(source, "ERROR", tb_short[:220])
    except Exception:
        pass

def install_global_exception_hook(source_name: str):
    def _hook(exc_type, exc_value, exc_tb):
        if issubclass(exc_type, KeyboardInterrupt):
            sys.__excepthook__(exc_type, exc_value, exc_tb)
            return
        report_fault(source_name, exc_value)
        sys.__excepthook__(exc_type, exc_value, exc_tb)
    sys.excepthook = _hook

def fault_tolerant(source_name: str, default_return=None):
    def decorator(func):
        @functools.wraps(func)
        def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
            try:
                return func(*args, **kwargs)
            except Exception as e:
                report_fault(source_name, e)
                return default_return
        return wrapper
    return decorator

"""
src/utils/tg_notifier.py
Lightweight Telegram Tier-3 Logging Group notifier for pipeline lifecycle events (Start / Stop / Crash).
"""
import os
import time
import shutil
import requests
from datetime import datetime
from dotenv import load_dotenv

current_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
project_root = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(current_dir, "../../"))
load_dotenv(os.path.join(project_root, ".env"))

BOT_TOKEN = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN", "").strip()
CHAT_ID = (
    os.getenv("TIER3_CHAT_ID")
    or os.getenv("TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID")
    or os.getenv("TELEGRAM_GROUP_ID")
    or ""
).strip()


def get_sys_snapshot() -> str:
    """Returns compact RAM & Disk status string for Telegram footers."""
    try:
        with open("/proc/meminfo", "r") as f:
            lines = {line.split(":")[0]: int(line.split()[1]) for line in f.readlines()[:5]}
        avail_mb = lines.get("MemAvailable", 0) // 1024
        total_mb = lines.get("MemTotal", 1) // 1024
        disk_free_gb = shutil.disk_usage(project_root).free / (1024**3)
        return f"RAM Free: {avail_mb}/{total_mb} MB | Disk: {disk_free_gb:.1f} GB"
    except Exception:
        return "System: Online"


def send_tg_event(stage: str, status: str, details: str = "") -> bool:
    """
    Sends a structured pipeline notification to TIER3_CHAT_ID.
    status: 'START' | 'SUCCESS' | 'WARN' | 'ERROR'
    """
    if not BOT_TOKEN or not CHAT_ID:
        print(f"[TG NOTIFIER SKIP] Missing TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN or TIER3_CHAT_ID in .env | {stage}: {status}")
        return False

    icons = {
        "START": "🟢 *STARTED*",
        "SUCCESS": "✅ *COMPLETED*",
        "WARN": "⚠️ *WARNING*",
        "ERROR": "🚨 *FAILED*"
    }
    badge = icons.get(status.upper(), "ℹ️ *UPDATE*")
    now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%d-%b %H:%M:%S IST")
    sys_info = get_sys_snapshot()

    text = (
        f"{badge} | *{stage}*\n"
        f"🕒 `{now_str}`\n"
    )
    if details:
        text += f"📋 {details}\n"
    text += f"💻 `{sys_info}`"

    url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{BOT_TOKEN}/sendMessage"
    for attempt in range(2):
        try:
            resp = requests.post(
                url,
                json={"chat_id": CHAT_ID, "text": text, "parse_mode": "Markdown"},
                timeout=8
            )
            if resp.status_code == 200:
                return True
            else:
                print(f"[TG WARN] HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[TG ERROR] Attempt {attempt+1}: {e}")
            time.sleep(1)
    return False


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    stg = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "SYSTEM CHECK"
    stat = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "SUCCESS"
    det = sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv) > 3 else "Tier-3 Telegram logging hooks online."
    send_tg_event(stg, stat, det)
